{"parentUuid":"994e56ad-0942-4cdf-a34f-28839a027d9e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q923EcASX7yHNn4ETvk9a1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Master Timeline\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Master Timeline of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## Ancient History\n     9→\n    10→- [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] — original twin souls, divine jealousy begins\n    11→- [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] founds Circle of the Murder ([[Mordred|Ramses]]) + [[Artificer's Guild]] ([[Xerxes]])\n    12→- [[Mordred|Ramses]] forces lichdom on [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]; she crystallizes as Demi-Lich in a phylactery\n    13→- [[Tygras]] makes a deal with an arch-fey from [[Vecna]]'s plane; creates a weapon to bridge worlds\n    14→- [[Ranar Surefoot]] steals the [[Diadem of Dawn]] during the weapon's use; the weapon backfires\n    15→- [[Tygras]]'s superweapon destroys [[uma]], creating the desert; [[Tygras]] sealed in [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    16→- The [[Matrim Pact]] formed: human sailors, gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves\n    17→- Covenants of Magic established after [[Tygras]]'s destruction — no being except the Alabaster Seat may possess such power again\n    18→- [[Kolyma]] (Last Curator) establishes Zodiac guardianship of the Zeniths\n    19→- The [[forgesworn]] order established; [[Thorin the Bronze|Thorin Bronzebeard]] builds the original Warforged army\n    20→\n    21→## Recent Past (Decades Before Campaign)\n    22→\n    23→- ~26 years ago: [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] born; parents make a deal with [[Reynardine]] for the philosopher's stone\n    24→- ~8 years ago: Nyx's dominant soul emerges; her sister taken; parents spurn Nyx\n    25→- ~7 years ago: [[Xyn]] born — the \"perfect vessel\" for the arch-fey's plan\n    26→- [[Mordred]] possesses [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle Hollingsworth]] (Gnome, NOT Goblin) at [[Forest's Zenith]]\n    27→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] founded as a soul-harvesting front\n    28→- [[Thorkon Fireforge]] betrays [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]] at [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]]'s urging\n    29→- [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] betrays [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]], hunts all [[forgesworn]]\n    30→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]] and [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] flee [[Vag'Mahar]]; [[Hlin Blunderbeat|Hlin]] dies giving birth to [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] and an older brother\n    31→- [[Blood Star]] kills [[Thorson Bronzebeard II]]\n    32→- [[Oblivion Wayne]] deposes her brother, unites all Drow under vampire rule\n    33→- [[Ebon Wayne]] captures [[Zodiac|Bat Zodiac]], discovers [[Spell Plague]]\n    34→- [[Vecna]] raises [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis Geth]] as Illithilich\n    35→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] corrupts the Elder Brain\n    36→- [[Ned'Zailok]] attacks [[Tower Bridge]]; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]]'s father [[Yeldan Liadon|Yeldan]] goes missing\n    37→- [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]] covers the evacuation; [[Varis Liadon|Varis]] enlists\n    38→- [[Ned'Zailok]] defeated after 18 months of fighting\n    39→\n    40→## Campaign Era (136 Chapters)\n    41→\n    42→### Act 1 (Chapters 0-13) — The Awakening\n    43→\n    44→| Ch. | Event |\n    45→|-----|-------|\n    46→| 0 | Party recruited into [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. [[Jorgen]] arrives pursued by kobolds. Sewer descent. |\n    47→| 1 | [[Dallor Dallorbill|Dallor]] pickpockets note from [[Jorgen]] — proof of setup. |\n    48→| 2 | [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]] revealed as [[Mordred]]. \"2,000 souls.\" [[Jorgen]] killed, becomes revenant. [[Sabine Ra]] freed from phylactery, sacrifices herself. Crystalline hearts deposited in party. |\n    49→| 3-5 | [[Artificer's Guild]] rescue. [[Xerxes]] exposition. Soul tether. |\n    50→| 6 | Dopplegangers. [[V-Mardov]] rift opens. [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from alternate reality. |\n    51→| 7-8 | [[Jorgen]] revenant pursues [[Finkle Hollingsworth|Finkle]]. [[Thornberry Shade]] ship. [[jafar]]/Sally intrigue. |\n    52→| 9 | First Prophecy ([[Ruth]]). |\n    53→| 11 | [[Golden Reach]] demons (Danelle/Markus) exposed at Festival of Light. [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth|Stalwart]]'s divine sense. |\n    54→| 12 | Second Prophecy (Hags). Pestilence discovered — brewed from children's souls. |\n    55→| 13 | Third Prophecy ([[Mia]]). [[Mia]] reveals she wiped party's memories. |\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2 (Chapters 14-40) — The Voyage\n    58→\n    59→| Ch. | Event |\n    60→|-----|-------|\n    61→| 17 | [[Nathan Yanagan]] (spy for [[jafar]]) aboard [[Thornberry Shade]]. Tiefling prejudice arc. |\n    62→| 19 | Soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]]. |\n    63→| 23 | Zodiac spirits revealed — they bet their immortality on the champions. |\n    64→| 24-25 | Tomb of the Last Curator. Four Zenith hieroglyphics. |\n    65→| 26 | [[Kelik Waystar]] revealed as air genasi prince. |\n    66→| 29 | Green Tides of St. Ashbury warning. |\n    67→| 30-31 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed. [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed. [[Xyn]] used as messenger. |\n    68→| 34 | [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter discovered inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe. [[forgesworn]] lore revealed. [[Nocturnus Wayne]] kills cousin Dusken, becomes Drider King. |\n    69→| 36 | [[Fen Darkeyes|Devin Darkeyes]] found enslaved by the Aboleth. |\n    70→| 37-38 | Dvinter Castle / Phoenix arc. [[forgesworn]] connections revealed. |\n    71→\n    72→### Act 3 (Chapters 41-86) — The Escalation\n    73→\n    74→| Ch. | Event |\n    75→|-----|-------|\n    76→| 42+ | Campaign splits into parallel tracks. |\n    77→| ~66 | [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]]'s Homunculus Thraalm killed. |\n    78→| ~82 | Attempted revival of [[Zodiac|Falak]] the serpent using captured Mahen. |\n    79→| 86 | Reunion. Battle of the Distillery. |\n    80→\n    81→### Act 4 (Chapters 87-136) — The Endgame\n    82→\n    83→| Ch. | Event |\n    84→|-----|-------|\n    85→| 103 | Second Reunion. TPK and revival via myconid network. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] carrying Kelemtor. |\n    86→| 104-105 | [[Reginald Providence]] killed (kenku in disguise). [[Anchor of War]] silenced. Therzadun thwarted. Silence/Timothy unmasked as Therzadun's vessel. |\n    87→| 106 | Declaration of War on gods. [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations — wanted to save the party. |\n    88→| 107 | Ragnarok. Warforged awakening. [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar created in image of [[Ashara Everstar]]. |\n    89→| 108 | Three races of elves' gods revealed. Casaval reveals [[Irethia]] (prototype), [[Riven Fen|Fen]] (warden), [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] (bane). [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    90→| 109 | [[Josh Coolbreeze]] cleanses [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]] of vampirism. [[lolth]]-[[Raven Queen]] deal revealed. [[Ghost-Eye Thorrin]] disappears. |\n    91→| 113 | [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] framed for Pestilence \"oil spill.\" [[rimefang]]'s last ride. |\n    92→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal — all planes converging into the Feywild. |\n    93→| 119 | Time paradox — [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] finds own dead body from 2 weeks ago, kickstarts his own heart. |\n    94→| 133 | [[Reynaldine]]'s true plan revealed: orchestrated everything to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel. Hearthstone exposed as triple agent. |\n    95→| 135 | Book of Shadows plot to open Harvest and Winter Zenith. [[Tiamat]] at Harvest Zenith. |\n    96→| 136 | [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]. Three specters: one into [[Nocturnus Wayne|Nocturnus]]. [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild via Stone Table sacrifice. |\n    97→\n    98→## Prophesied: Gard'Daemon\n    99→\n   100→All four Zeniths held simultaneously. [[V-Mardov|V-MORDRED]]'s invasion repelled. The final configuration of Controllers, Champions, and Anchor Wielders activates.\n   101→\n   102→\n   103→## The True History Of Mardov (Extracted from PPTX)\n   104→\n   105→### Slide 1\n   106→\n   107→The True History Of\n   108→\n   109→---\n   110→\n   111→### Slide 2\n   112→\n   113→Year 0 - The Rose of Creation\n   114→\n   115→The Rose of Creation was the solution to the ever present conflict between Gods of Destruction and Gods of Creation. It is the culmination of countless Gods and Goddesses’ life's work, and is thusly an irresistible target for Destruction. One such petal on the Rose belonged to The Raven Queen of Fate who made a Pact with Vecna to twin their worlds to their mutual benefit.\n   116→\n   117→---\n   118→\n   119→### Slide 3\n   120→\n   121→The Death Pact\n   122→\n   123→The shared domain of Death would serve as the cornerstone of their agreement. This Pact is largely a mystery, however their specialties point to some grand revelation that will occur at the Fated time of the Raven Queen’s transition from Life to Death. This harvest of the limitless potential of the regent Goddess would culminate in a shared Secret with Vecna, God of Death and Secrets. The end result is a mutually beneficial understanding of What is Beyond the Death of Gods? Each believing they would best the other at the moment of the revelation as they were better equipped to take the advantage, the Pact was sealed.\n   124→\n   125→Mardov and Vodram, twinned worlds, were formed and with them 4 Zeniths capable of wielding the power of the domain of Death.\n   126→\n   127→---\n   128→\n   129→### Slide 4\n   130→\n   131→Zeniths\n   132→\n   133→From the Mountains to the Forest, in Winter or in Harvest, the Zeniths of power await their Anchor and Heart.\n   134→\n   135→There will be War and Death, Pestilence and Famine, but to Anchor the end of Guard’ Daemon, the battlefield will be tallied, its victor declared.\n   136→\n   137→For they wield a secret from beyond the grave of gods. Even in the fires of the Forge or the shadows of the Wilde, the light of Faith and the hope of Progenitors shall strike their Fated Heart.\n   138→\n   139→---\n   140→\n   141→### Slide 5\n   142→\n   143→Bringer of Oblivion\n   144→\n   145→Asmodeus, Bringer of Oblivion only took 100 years to find Mardov, and 12 months to signal the first Guard’Daemon. However, to truely seal Asmodeus away, Kolyma, the First and Last Curator of Mardov had to sacrifice himself to activate the Zeniths and turn them against Oblivion. Unable to fully destroy the God, Asmodeus was sealed in thirteen amulets, to be scattered to the guardians on the edges of existence.\n   146→\n   147→---\n   148→\n   149→### Slide 6\n   150→\n   151→Inheritors of the Mantle\n   152→\n   153→In the Death of Kolyma, the potential he captured to answer the Curator’s Plea was bestowed in four mantles.\n   154→\n   155→The Alabaster Mantle of Arcane Magic\n   156→\n   157→The Ivy Mantle of Natural Magic\n   158→\n   159→The Palladium Mantle of Divine Magic\n   160→\n   161→The Crystalline Mantle of Mortal Magic\n   162→"}]},"uuid":"b056eb77-ce76-472b-815f-f6f146fd558e","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:31.521Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"994e56ad-0942-4cdf-a34f-28839a027d9e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"e8a2a1ac-0cfa-4b40-818a-604555c7ddb5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01XYcmGp2U92VqSm9ZmooE8W","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Story Arcs & Narrative Structure\n     7→\n     8→## The Overarching Story\n     9→\n    10→The campaign revolves around [[Mordred|Ramses/Mordred]], whose plan unfolds in three stages:\n    11→\n    12→1. **Gets the Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] to sacrifice her immortality** -- this creates a rift between [[Mardov]] and [[V-Mardov]] where [[Vecna]]'s Avatar rules\n    13→2. **Collects [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s gifts to control her power** -- originally wanted to resurrect [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]], his lost love, but after her spirit shattered he pivoted to controlling her fragments. Gave up his phylactery to take out the [[Artificer's Guild]], gaining [[Xerxes]]'s body (corrupting the individual beings that make up [[Xerxes]])\n    14→3. **Destroys all the keystones** -- with his newfound body, [[Mordred]] has one goal: bring about the invasion of this realm by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones\n    15→\n    16→### The Keystones\n    17→\n    18→| Name | Gemstone | Location | Conflict Type | Horseman/Anchor |\n    19→|------|----------|----------|---------------|-----------------|\n    20→| [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Forest's Zenith]] | Dungeon | Death |\n    21→| [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Mountain's Zenith]] | Dwarven underdark conflict | War |\n    22→| [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Winter's Zenith]] | Winter's wasteland | Pestilence |\n    23→| [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Harvest's Zenith]] | Cataclysmic, philosopher's stone | Famine |\n    24→\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→## Act I — The Awakening (Chapters 0-13)\n    28→\n    29→The party is recruited into the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]]. They encounter [[Mordred]] possessing [[Finkle Hollingsworth]], [[Sabine Ra]] is freed from her phylactery, and crystalline heart fragments are deposited in the party. Three prophecies are received from [[Ruth]], the Hags, and [[Mia]], establishing the four-zenith framework.\n    30→\n    31→**Key beats:**\n    32→- Party death and resurrection by [[Sabine Ra]] (Chapter 2)\n    33→- [[V-Mardov]] rift opens; [[Varis Liadon|Varris]] arrives from the alternate reality (Chapter 6)\n    34→- First, Second, and Third Prophecies received (Chapters 9, 12, 13)\n    35→- [[Mia]] reveals she wiped the party's memories\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## Act II — The Voyage (Chapters 14-40)\n    40→\n    41→The party sails aboard the [[Thornberry Shade]], encounters the zodiac spirits (who bet their immortality on the champions), explores the [[Tomb of the Last Curator]], and splits into parallel tracks.\n    42→\n    43→**Key beats:**\n    44→- Tiefling prejudice arc; soul glamours for [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] (Chapters 17-19)\n    45→- Zodiac spirits revealed; they bet their immortality on the champions (Chapter 23)\n    46→- Tomb of the Last Curator; four zenith hieroglyphics decoded (Chapters 24-25)\n    47→- [[Peraxis Phyrexia]] revealed; [[Reynaldine]]'s network exposed (Chapters 30-31)\n    48→- [[Thorkon Fireforge|Thorkon]]'s letter found inside [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]]'s axe; [[forgesworn]] lore revealed (Chapter 34)\n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→\n    52→## Act III — The Escalation (Chapters 41-86)\n    53→\n    54→Campaign splits into parallel tracks. Individual character arcs deepen.\n    55→\n    56→### The Cloud City Arc ([[windhaven]])\n    57→\n    58→The party arrives in [[windhaven]] via a portal from the [[Kraken of the Sand Seas]]. Key events:\n    59→\n    60→1. **[[Tiberius D. Rex]]** receives a premonition of the party's arrival\n    61→2. **Research at the Windmill Willow**: [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] researches the zodiacs (astral chart showing the 12 animals, one per month); [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] researches the [[Diadem of Dawn]]; [[Randal]] researches the gods\n    62→3. **[[Leonidas of Summer's Last Light]]** puts the party on trial in his sphinx demi-plane:\n    63→   - [[Varis Liadon|Varys]] requests help against [[Vecna]], is granted a vision of [[Daina Fireforge|Daina]] grabbing the [[Heart of the Forge]] and jumping into lava\n    64→   - [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] requests help to save [[Xyn]], meets [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]] -- her real patron\n    65→   - [[Fen Darkeyes|Fen]] wishes he and his brother never fell under the Aboleth's influence; wish granted\n    66→   - [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] alone is deemed unworthy (meaning unknown)\n    67→   - [[Tiberius D. Rex|Tiberious]] has undergone this process 75 times, making him far older than the 76 he appears\n    68→4. **The Cabal of Horns**: [[Randal]] and [[Tesca Duskhallow|Tesca]] discover an underground tiefling society led by [[Winnipeg Redvine]], uncovering a child-kidnapping plot by Lady [[Mathia Nuum]]\n    69→5. **Reynaldine's Demand**: The party encounters [[Reynaldine|Reynaldine of the Autumn Court]] collecting Elemental Seals. [[Kelik Temptus]] dies when absorbed into his amulet. [[Reynaldine]] already has Fire and Earth seals; tasks the party with retrieving the Water amulet from [[Lapis Poseidon]]\n    70→6. **[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s Stag Form**: Discovers her stag form resembles [[Shi-Shi-Gami|Shi Shi Gami]], with antlers forming the [[Zodiac Locket]]\n    71→7. **Cloud City Falling**: The party fights to save Cloud City, encounters [[Zepheeron the Bronze]], battles at Jackfall Landing, and is rescued by [[Captain Liza]] on the [[Thornberry Wilde]]\n    72→8. **[[Reynaldine]]'s True Nature Revealed**: [[Randal]] uses Legend Lore and discovers [[Reynaldine]] is actually 4 beings: [[Juliannia of the Summer Court]], [[Graz'zt|Grazz't the Demon Prince]], [[Gabriel the Kind]] (Archangel), and [[Reynaldine|Reynardine, Prince of Harvest]]\n    73→9. **[[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] meets [[Xyn]]**: Via Lorelai's magical broach, the twin sisters finally communicate. [[Xyn]] reveals [[Gabriel the Kind]] is her patron (lives in teddy bear \"Mr. Happy\"), that the [[Mountain's Zenith]] opening released [[Barachiel the Righteous]], and that [[Euclid]]'s book *The Origin of Genasi and the Rebirth of the Progenitors* is the key to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n    74→10. **The Harvest's Zenith spotted**: [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]] touches the satellite and sees a hanging upside-down city with the [[Harvest's Zenith]] -- a dual pyramid with 7 floors all rotating out of phase\n    75→\n    76→---\n    77→\n    78→## Act IV — The Endgame (Chapters 87-136)\n    79→\n    80→The threads converge. Character arcs resolve. Gods die. The world changes.\n    81→\n    82→**Key beats:**\n    83→- Second Reunion; TPK and revival via myconid network (Chapter 103)\n    84→- [[Reginald Providence]] killed; [[Anchor of War]] silenced; Therzadun thwarted (Chapters 104-105)\n    85→- Declaration of War on gods; [[Peraxis Phyrexia|Peraxis]] reveals complex motivations (Chapter 106)\n    86→- Ragnarok; Warforged awakening; [[Irethia]] revealed as aasimar (Chapter 107)\n    87→- [[lolth]]'s \"Two Truths and a Lie\" (Chapter 108)\n    88→- [[Asmodeus]] killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]]; three specters escape, one enters [[Nocturnus Wayne]] (Chapter 136)\n    89→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of [[Mardov]]'s [[Feywild]] via Stone Table sacrifice (Chapter 136)\n    90→\n    91→---\n    92→\n    93→## Global Conflicts\n    94→\n    95→### The Orc-Human War ([[DawnHaven]])\n    96→\n    97→**Location:** [[dawnpass]]/[[windhaven]]/[[Eavallan]] vs [[Stra'Franar]]/[[Fae'ish]]/[[Tygras Logath]]\n    98→\n    99→**Reveal:** At the [[Magister's Circle]], an envoy from [[dawnpass]] (notably containing zero Half-Orcs -- there have been none in the entire game) requests aid against an army of sorcerers wielding draconic-blood-fueled destruction similar to the Tiefling army that destroyed [[uma]].\n   100→\n   101→**Root cause:** The Orc Chieftain and former Gladiator Pit Champion ([[Logath HellEye|Logath]]) made a deal with the same fae demon as [[Tygras]], causing intense bloodlust and madness. This makes him a target for [[Mordred]], who uses Logath's influence to cut off food from the fertile Harvestlands north of [[windhaven]].\n   102→\n   103→**Connection to the Zenith:** This is the location of the [[Anchor of Famine]], tied to the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. The resurgence of Orc sorcerers matches exactly with when it last rained. The [[Matrim Pact]] was between human sailors of old [[uma]], gnomes of [[Fae'ish]], and [[Qual'Xar]] half-elves -- they promised sanctuary atop the [[Maturin Turtle]].\n   104→\n   105→**The Hidden City:** The city is actually a buried pyramid, the great ziggurat housing the [[Harvest's Zenith]]. Inside, [[Tygras]] himself (or the arcane being he became after his failed ascension) has bid his time feeding on internal zenith energies. Destroying his echo rips a hole in the Fae and reveals the fae demon who gave [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] her powers.\n   106→\n   107→**Nyx's Choice:** The demon was the one who made the pact with [[Tygras]], and [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] was his heir. By destroying the last echo of [[Tygras]], future tiefling children can be prevented. [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]] inherits the mark of [[Tygras]] (actually Sable's Beacon, an ancient fae marking). She can either rid the world of \"demon children\" or rebuild the lost empire by adding tieflings to the [[Matrim Pact]] and reviving the guardian turtle.\n   108→\n   109→**Reviving the Guardian:** Requires (1) removing the [[Anchor of Famine]] from the top of the zenith (guarded by the Harvest Effigy), (2) returning the [[Heart of the Progenitors]] to the crevasse in the middle of [[Tygras Logath]], (3) speaking the words: *\"On his back all vows are made, a haven for the continent of dawn paid.\"* Upon finishing, the turtle skeleton below the bay is revived -- a massive turtle carrying an Atlantean city, serving as THE landing place between [[Mardov]] and the [[Feywild]].\n   110→\n   111→---\n   112→\n   113→## Character Arc Themes\n   114→\n   115→### [[Sprocket Overspark]] — The Pursuit of Fun\n   116→\n   117→**Question:** Is it possible for responsibility to be fun?\n   118→\n   119→[[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]'s fate as the next Avatar of the [[Raven Queen]] cannot be downplayed. No matter how he tries, this is his overarching conflict: *\"You cannot run from your fate.\"* To learn this is to understand what it means to become the next [[Raven Queen]].\n   120→\n   121→His rise of infamy: Trixie -> [[Thornberry Shade]] -> [[Qual'Xar]] -> [[Magister's Circle]] & [[Conquis Abbey]] -> Entire Material Plane. The deeds he deems \"fun\" provide notoriety, making it harder to be underestimated.\n   122→\n   123→### [[Brigit Wildfire]] — Self-Discovery\n   124→\n   125→**Question:** What makes me special?\n   126→\n   127→[[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]] is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]] -- one of the original beings created in Eden. This grants her the ability to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]] without destroying the elemental seals. There is no reason for this other than fate -- the Progenitors were fated to return to help stop [[Gard'Daemon]].\n   128→\n   129→### [[Randal]] — Apotheosis and Pacifism\n   130→\n   131→**Question:** What does it take to become a god?\n   132→\n   133→[[Randal]]'s decision to become a pacifist is valiant but ultimately flawed, for he claimed victory while he still has goals outstanding. His ideal: *\"Victory is only satisfying if achieved without violence.\"*\n   134→\n   135→Foils to his pacifism:\n   136→- *\"Violence is Victory\"* -- [[Logath HellEye|Logath]], Orcish Leader\n   137→- *\"Peace is what I will achieve after all my enemies have been defeated by my hand\"* -- [[Evelyandrin of the Spring Court]]\n   138→- *\"Some things are worth fighting for\"* -- [[Reynaldine]]\n   139→- *\"Nothing is worth fighting for, I will endure and adapt\"* -- [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n   140→\n   141→---\n   142→\n   143→## Player Background Tie-Ins\n   144→\n   145→### [[Sprocket Overspark]]\n   146→- Has a yurt outside [[hinderstap]] village in [[monethern]]\n   147→- Sent by [[lillith]], the Platinum Seat of the Diviners Covenant, to investigate a village where inhabitants are normal by day but ravenous and murderous by night\n   148→- Being killed in the village forces the murdered to stay permanently, reborn each morning (happened to Giddean Clary, Mustard Bard, with his Basset Hound Darling)\n   149→- A portal/wound links [[hinderstap]] to the [[shadowfell]]\n   150→\n   151→### [[Brigit Wildfire]]\n   152→- Criminal contact: [[Firekeeper Morn Loregramar|Firekeeper Lorgrammar]], orc shaman whose people betrayed him and assaulted [[Eavallan]] Forest, [[Stra'Franar]], and Last Tower Bridge\n   153→- She is a [[Progenitors|Progenitor]], one of the original beings created in Eden\n   154→- [[Eavallan]] Forest is of the [[Feywild]], the earliest location where elves first came to [[Mardov]]\n   155→- The original sacking of [[Eavallan]] by the orcs set into motion the steps to open the [[Harvest's Zenith]]\n   156→- Progenitor blood was thought to be in [[uma]], but the bloodline was still strong; [[Brigit Wildfire|Brigitte]]'s parents were drawn to [[Eavallan]] because it was closest to the Zenith\n   157→\n   158→---\n   159→\n   160→## Story Beats Still To Hit\n   161→\n   162→### [[Randal]] and Apotheosis\n   163→- The gods were the first to uncover universal truths\n   164→- Once a truth is uncovered, Apotheosis becomes possible (see [[lore/cosmology|Cosmology]])\n   165→- [[Reynaldine]]'s plan to collapse all planes is his miracle attempt\n   166→\n   167→### The Raven Queen's Transition\n   168→- The Death Pact between the [[Raven Queen]] and [[Vecna]] hinges on the Raven Queen's transition from Life to Death\n   169→- The harvest of the regent Goddess's limitless potential will culminate in a shared Secret with [[Vecna]]\n   170→- Both believe they will best the other at the moment of revelation\n   171→"}]},"uuid":"0032285a-6379-4a98-af6a-eef08b88652e","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:31.952Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e8a2a1ac-0cfa-4b40-818a-604555c7ddb5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→\n    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→\n    13→---\n    14→\n    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→\n    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→\n    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→\n    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. Each character's journey is tracked chronologically through party splits, reunions, and pivotal moments.\n    10→    10→\n    11→    11→> **Confidence Legend**: [HIGH] = directly from player notes or handouts. [MEDIUM] = inferred from structural evidence. [LOW] = reconstructed from context.\n    12→    12→\n    13→    13→---\n    14→    14→\n    15→    15→## BRIGIT (Wild Spirit of Fire)\n    16→    16→\n    17→    17→**Player**: Liz\n    18→    18→**Class**: Druid (Circle of the Moon) / Barbarian multiclass\n    19→    19→**Race**: Changeling/Shapeshifter\n    20→    20→**Key Items**: Anchor of Death (locket), Stag Zodiac\n    21→    21→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n    22→    22→\n    23→    23→### Arc Summary\n    24→    24→\n    25→    25→Brigit begins as a chaotic, multi-formed druid struggling with the many voices in her head. She becomes a Progenitor through a ritual in Eden with the Children of the Forest, gains custody of the Anchor of Death in her locket, and carries the Stag Zodiac. Her arc is one of identity -- learning to channel her rage and multiplicity into purpose. She is the Wild Spirit of Fire, and through her shapeshifting, serves as the party's most versatile combatant and emotional anchor. By Act 4 she has ascended the locket, lost her elemental powers during the Harvest Zenith battle, and must find her way forward without her primary coping mechanism.\n    26→    26→\n    27→    27→### [HIGH] Discovers Forest Zenith's state |\n    77→    77→| 136 | Battlefield: locket pulsating with acrid green (usually emerald), rain of ash at Machu, elemental powers gone | [HIGH] Power loss -- voices gone, alone in her head for first time |\n    78→    78→| 136 | Speaks with dead on black dragon; learns Vecna is on Mardov, wants Death Zenith | [HIGH] Critical intelligence |\n    79→    79→\n    80→    80→### Turning Points\n    81→    81→\n    82→    82→1. **Death and Revival (Ch 69T)**: First real death, revived by party magic. Establishes mortality stakes.\n    83→    83→2. **Becoming a Progenitor (Ch 82T)**: Transforms from adventurer to something more -- connected to the land itself.\n    84→    84→3. **The Rage Speech (Ch 127)**: \"Do you have any idea how many voices exist in my head at any given time?\" -- defines her identity.\n    85→    85→4. **Power Loss (Ch 135-136)**: Elemental magic stripped at Harvest Zenith. The silence in her head is deafening.\n    86→    86→\n    87→    87→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n    88→    88→\n    89→    89→- Alive, powers stripped (no elemental/vegan magic)\n    90→    90→- Anchor of Death ascended but forest zenith situation unclear\n    91→    91→- Locket pulsating abnormally (acrid green vs usual emerald)\n    92→    92→- Must find Forest Zenith and confront zombie Finkle + demi-lich phylactery\n    93→    93→- Needs her powers turned back on\n    94→    94→\n    95→    95→---\n    96→    96→\n    97→    97→## SPROCKET OVERSPARK\n    98→    98→\n    99→    99→**Player**: (unknown)\n   100→   100→**Class**: Wizard (School of Divination)\n   101→   101→**Race**: Gnome\n   102→   102→**Key Items**: Third Eye (ethereal sight), Portal Gun, Robe of the Archmagi, Mordred's cursed spellbook\n   103→   103→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n   104→   104→\n   105→   105→### Arc Summary\n   106→   106→\n   107→   107→Sprocket is the party's divination wizard who becomes embroiled in a cosmic chess match. Cursed by Mordred's spellbook, nearly framed as a necromancer, he rises to become the Alabaster Seat (the conduit of all arcane magic on Mardov). His third eye gives him unique sight into the ethereal plane, and his crystallization is slowly killing him. He becomes the Knight of Spring/Flower Field, and his journey is about bearing impossible burdens with humor and stubborn gnomish resolve. \"The Gnome Yoinks\" is his signature move.\n   108→   108→\n   109→   109→### Timeline\n   110→   110→\n   111→   111→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   112→   112→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   113→   113→| 0-41 | United party adventures; establishes divination specialty | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   114→   114→| 49T | Cloud City: identifies items, spends 300 electrum from Zephyron, investigates the Roost | [HIGH] Cloud City exploration |\n   115→   115→| 64T | Runs brewing operations (\"Sprocket runs the shots\"), fails to recognize Randall's song about him | [HIGH] Character moment |\n   116→   116→| 69T | Fights Necromancer: Disintegrates Volstead (Skeletor) with portent roll, saves party from total defeat | [HIGH] Signature disintegrate + portent combo established |\n   117→   117→| 72T-76T | Alabaster tournament: cursed by Mordred's spellbook, framed as necromancer by Leon, defended by Randall in trial | [HIGH] Major political/personal crisis |\n   118→   118→| 76T | Trial: spellbook revealed as Mordred's phylactery-like artifact, Leon exposed as true necromancer | [HIGH] Vindication, but new danger |\n   119→   119→| 77T | Leon summons 12 Morkoths in courtroom, party fights through, Leon is killed by Fen | [HIGH] Trial conclusion |\n   120→   120→| 78T | Beholder trial: teams with Fen and Oswald, uses Disintegrate+Portent then Phantasmal Killer to defeat beholder | [HIGH] Classic Sprocket tactics |\n   121→   121→| 79T | Feywilde: revealed as emissary of Winter through rabbit zodiac connection, gives one drop of blood in secret vote | [HIGH] Cosmic political role |\n   122→   122→| 82T | Final trials: dominates Ashara with portent, throws match to help Anemone become Alabaster Seat | [HIGH] Selfless act -- lets Anemone win |\n   123→   123→| 82T | Anemone modifies Sprocket's memory so he doesn't remember choosing to lose | [HIGH] Bittersweet victory |\n   124→   124→| 83T | Becomes Platinum Seat of Divination; explores his new office (globe map of Mardov) | [HIGH] New role |\n   125→   125→| 84T | Sends messages to Fen and Evan; discovers sending stone spy device in tower | [HIGH] Intelligence operations |\n   126→   126→| 85T | Controls weather to aid Battle of the Distillery; uses Earthquake scroll | [HIGH] Battlefield control |\n   127→   127→| 86 | Reunion: Disintegrates Dallor with portent, fires tower weapons | [HIGH] End of Act 3 |\n   128→   128→| 88-89 | Investigates tower infiltration, discovers teleportation circle in blood beneath tiles | [HIGH] Security breach |\n   129→   129→| 90 | Falls under dream spell via Mlemph (Kuo-Toa); vision of teleportation circle's purpose | [HIGH] Prophetic vision |\n   130→   130→| 90 | Dreams of Valley of Anchors destruction: beam of blood-red light, 6-fingered hooded figure, Gar'daemon | [HIGH] Foresight of endgame |\n   131→   131→| 100 | Shadowfell; time dilation confusion | [HIGH] Planar adventure |\n   132→   132→| 107 | Becomes Alabaster Seat officially (Ch 107/111 confirmed) | [HIGH] Full power assumption |\n   133→   133→| 112 | Crystallization worsening; portal experiments; \"Rube GoldBERGS\" | [HIGH] Physical deterioration |\n   134→   134→| 125 | Becomes Knight of Spring (replacing Tezka who lost magic) | [HIGH] Seasonal court role |\n   135→   135→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey: maintains anti-magic field throughout battle, writes \"Narcissa and Octavious are Frauds\" in chalk at intersection of all three circles | [HIGH] Tactical genius |\n   136→   136→| 128 | Nearly killed by Octavious; Raven Queen brings him back personally: \"you have wonderful friends\" | [HIGH] Direct divine intervention |\n   137→   137→| 128 | Third eye visible to everyone, crying purple tears, \"The Gnome Yoinks\" -- rips cloak/wings off Octavious with Bigsby's Hand | [HIGH] Signature moment |\n   138→   138→| 130 | Cloud City: creates minor illusion of Keelick's voice to lure guards, finds 10th Wind in ethereal plane | [HIGH] Reconnaissance |\n   139→   139→| 131 | Holds the shot with Varis on Nautilus railgun controls | [HIGH] Teamwork |\n   140→   140→| 132 | Stone Table: casts Hold Monster on Gabriel, counterspells Grat'z multiple times, takes spring brazier position | [HIGH] Critical role in ritual |\n   141→   141→| 133 | Harvest: walks in counterclockwise circles to reverse time effects, apologizes for gambling with Bergs' life | [HIGH] Self-awareness moment |\n   142→   142→| 135 | Divinity: Snowket casts lightning at Asmodeus; Sprocket blinds, shoots portal gun, counterspells Tiamat's meteor storm | [HIGH] Maximum wizard output |\n   143→   143→| 135-136 | Sees three spectres leave Asmodeus: one stays (shade), one goes up, one enters Nocturnus | [HIGH] Ethereal sight revelation |\n   144→   144→\n   145→   145→### Turning Points\n   146→   146→\n   147→   147→1. **The Framing (Ch 72T-76T)**: Mordred's spellbook curse nearly destroys his reputation and life.\n   148→   148→2. **Letting Anemone Win (Ch 82T)**: Selfless sacrifice of the Alabaster Seat.\n   149→   149→3. **The Gnome Yoinks (Ch 128)**: Signature moment of power and defiance.\n   150→   150→4. **Counterspelling Tiamat (Ch 135)**: Counters meteor storm, saves the entire party.\n   151→   151→\n   152→   152→### Status (as of Ch 136)\n   153→   153→\n   154→   154→- Alive, crystallization ongoing\n   155→   155→- Alabaster Seat / Knight of Spring\n   156→   156→- Third eye attuned to ethereal plane\n   157→   157→- Portal gun (stats still pending from DM)\n   158→   158→- Powers likely affected by zenith magic changes\n   159→   159→\n   160→   160→---\n   161→   161→\n   162→   162→## VARIS\n   163→   163→\n   164→   164→**Player**: (unknown)\n   165→   165→**Class**: Ranger (with portal/planar abilities via crystalline heart)\n   166→   166→**Race**: Elf (Eladrin connection)\n   167→   167→**Key Items**: Curator's Plea (bow), Horn (weapon), Ring of Air Elemental\n   168→   168→**Track**: Tieflings -> Distillery -> Good Guys Guild\n   169→   169→\n   170→   170→### Arc Summary\n   171→   171→\n   172→   172→Varis is the party's ranger and moral compass -- a survivor of Vodram's fall who carries deep guilt about those he couldn't save. His crystalline heart grants him portal abilities, and his connection to the Autumn Court through Reynardine defines his political role. He wields the Curator's Plea and the Horn, channeling the legacy of fallen civilizations. His arc is about duty, the weight of command, and finding purpose after loss. By Act 4, he becomes Knight of Autumn and bears an almost unbearable burden of responsibility.\n   173→   173→\n   174→   174→### Timeline\n   175→   175→\n   176→   176→| Chapter | Event | Significance |\n   177→   177→|---------|-------|-------------|\n   178→   178→| 0-41 | United party; survivor of Vodram | [MEDIUM] Foundation |\n   179→   179→| 49T | Cloud City: goes invisible to infiltrate, encounters various NPCs at the tavern (Sam, Matthew Windsmith, Itori) | [HIGH] Diplomatic/intelligence role |\n   180→   180→| 64T | Taste-tests brewing but fails (too drunk), meets beige ninja (air genasi) | [HIGH] Character moment |\n   181→   181→| 69T | Fights Necromancer: 77-damage critical with lightning bowstring, goes down twice from Finger of Death | [HIGH] Nearly dies fighting Ned'Zailok |\n   182→   182→| 69T | Vision: sees himself in a tower with the Necromancer by a fire; spirit of stag enters | [HIGH] Mystical connection to antagonist |\n   183→   183→| 72T-76T | Tournament: enters Mage's Prison, nearly killed by Morkoths, rescued by Randall | [HIGH] Vulnerable moment |\n   184→   184→| 78T | Beholder trial: bow destroyed by Death Ray, fights with sword and chain lightning | [HIGH] Adapts to loss |\n   185→   185→| 79T | Feywilde: recognized as autumn emissary, sacrifices blood in the vote, feels duty hit \"like a ton of bricks\" | [HIGH] Seasonal court identity |\n   186→   186→| 82T | Votes in 6th trial, teaches funeral customs | [MEDIUM] Cultural role |\n   187→   187→| 83T | Buys 2 superior, 2 greater, 12 regular healing potions; sets up refugee operations at keep | [HIGH] Logistics |\n   188→   188→| 85T | Arrives at Distillery via Kuo-Toa boat (sent by Reynardine), praised as hero on arrival | [HIGH] Homecoming |\n   189→   189→| 86 | Reunion: Guardian of Nature form, teaches funeral customs for fallen, builds memorial | [HIGH] Leadership |\n   190→   190→| 87T | Organizes keep operations, discovers portals in wells and at Sprocket's tower | [HIGH] Security operations |\n   191→   191→| 88-89 | Takes stock of damage, mentors Iris (promoted to Countess), rebuilds keep | [HIGH] Commander role |\n   192→   192→| 90 | Uses zombies as necromantic detectors, discovers portal to autumn feywilde | [HIGH] Tactical innovation |\n   193→   193→| 100-103 | Shadowfell and reunion adventures | [MEDIUM] Continuing journey |\n   194→   194→| 116 | Convergence and Betrayal | [HIGH] Major plot event |\n   195→   195→| 125 | Retrieves final Asmodeus seal | [HIGH] Endgame quest completion |\n   196→   196→| 127-128 | Conquis Abbey: phases out of world to avoid sorcerers, channels Horn and Curator's Plea, chains lightning, kills sorcerers with force shots through throats | [HIGH] Peak combat performance |\n   197→   197→| 128 | \"In the back: I have witnessed the fall of Vodram... through me their legacy endures\" -- hears Curator's Plea speak | [HIGH] Legacy connection deepens |\n   198→   198→| 128 | Contacted by Gabriel/Reynardine during Oblivion experience: \"I can't believe you're here. You came to save me.\" | [HIGH] Spiritual connection |\n   199→   199→| 130 | Cloud City: follows Keelick to protect him, casts Protection from Evil and Good on the king | [HIGH] Protective instincts |\n   200→   200→| 130 | Takes guardian of nature form, erupts feral scream \"go home and protect your families,\" uses windwall to disperse mob | [HIGH] Leadership under pressure |"}]},"uuid":"259b3739-dbaf-471c-abaf-50cacc60b7ec","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:40.578Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3d7304b1-1dae-4d22-bf80-303e7626d742","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"3d214ae2-3f64-44fe-a679-07cbff05b260","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NDaizB7twikxGn2oqtwBwL","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: dm-note\n     3→title: \"Cloud City Arc\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/Notes/Cloud City Arc.docx\"]\n     5→category: \"\"\n     6→tags: []\n     7→---\n     8→\n     9→- The party arrived in WindHaven via a portal opened by the **Kraken of the Sand Seas** to one of the Qual'Xar Vessels, **the Flooded Roots**, moored at the port safely tucked away inside the cliff face where the rest of the city rests\n    10→\n    11→- The party headed up the switchbacks up to the city proper and were greeted by **Tiberious D Rex** who had received a premonition that the party would be passing through there\n    12→\n    13→- The party headed to the center of town where the massive Windmill Willow grows, meeting Kelik Temptus, crown prince of the wind genasi, who needed a bit more time to get ready to make the journey through the everstorm surrounding WindHaven\n    14→\n    15→- The party then ventured into the library held within the truck of the tree for some research and shopping\n    16→\n    17→  - Brigit researched the zodiacs\n    18→\n    19→    - grabs the astral chart that\\'s been transcribed over a year.\n    20→\n    21→      - Not all of them depict the 12, but on the first day of each month, there\\'s a different animal.\n    22→\n    23→      - Current Month is the Rabbit, Nivens, and then next is the Pangolin, Osiris!\n    24→\n    25→  - Tesca researches her hometown\n    26→\n    27→    - the History of Dawnpass \\<about the founding of the village\\>\n    28→\n    29→    - the Origin of the Dawn \\<about the creation of the diadem of the dawn\\>\n    30→\n    31→  - Randal researched the gods\n    32→\n    33→    - He also got a recipe for preventing and curing dementia \\<all ingredients must be consumed at the same time\\>\n    34→\n    35→  - Nyx was confronted due to her connection with an entity that she is not familiar\n    36→\n    37→- While the others were doing some research, Sprocket confronted a scroll shopkeep and decided to attempt to mind-control the condescending librarian which caused the entire tree's worth of halflings to descend on the party\n    38→\n    39→- After getting chased out of the tree by a mob of angry \"squirrels\", the party sought refuge with Kelik who created a barrier to house the party, but a large sphinx descended from the top of the willow\n    40→\n    41→- The sphinx teleported the party to his demi-plane housed within the leaves of trees and announced himself as **Leonidas of Summer's Last Light**, the party was then put on trial\n    42→\n    43→  - The trial consisted of 3 challenges which were denoted by the three roars of the sphinx, however only the first trial was meant to determine innocence, the second was to denote worthyness and the third was only completed by Fen Darkeyes who was granted a wish through time\n    44→\n    45→    - Varys requested help in the fight against Vecna\n    46→\n    47→      - He also is granted a vision of the present where he sees **Daina Ironforge** grab a red gem \\<Heart of the Forge\\> and jump into a pool of lava\n    48→\n    49→    - Nyx requested help to save her sister, Xyn\n    50→\n    51→      - She is granted a chat with a blonde woman with warm colored leaves all over her body like clothing. She has a summery voice, this is **Juliannia of the Summer Court**, her real patron\\...\n    52→\n    53→    - Fen asked that he and his brother had never fallen under the influence of the Aboleth who had torn them apart and forced him to kill his brother, this request was granted, though circumstances that led to him joining the party remained, his brother, **Devin BrightHallow** is supposedly still in Monethern City working as the captain of **The Verdant Horizon**\n    54→\n    55→  - Stalwart who had suffered from a serious advancement in age of 14 years requested that for his boon he was returned to his normal age safe and sound... he headed to the Traveler's Pony for a pint\n    56→\n    57→  - Of the worthy Stalwart, Nyx, Varys, Tiberious, and eventually (after completing the second trial) Fen\n    58→\n    59→  - Sprocket alone was left unworthy, though the meaning remains a mystery...\n    60→\n    61→    - He did however later dimension door out of the pocket dimension... and go get a pint at the Traveler's Pony as well\n    62→\n    63→  - Tiberious was the only member to accompany Fen in the third trial, it was revealed that he had undergone this process 75 times ... making him much older than the 76 he appeared to be, he refused to answer more questions\n    64→\n    65→    - Ultimately he succumbed and backed out of the third trial crying for \"Juliania\" (is this the **Juliania of the Summer Court**, and Nyx's Patron?)\n    66→\n    67→- Meanwhile, Randal and Tesca were exploring the Traveler's Pony and uncovered that it was a hideout for the **Cabal of Horns**, an underground society of Tieflings who were led by a scarlet tiefling named: **Winnepeg Redvine**\n    68→\n    69→  - The two were warned of a plot by a secret society who had been kidnapping children and shipping them to Dawnpass, the local lord **Mathia Nuum** was providing the front, her symbol is: A Red Sailed Fishing Vessel\n    70→\n    71→  - There was a pale tiefling who had been recently rescued, however Randal and Tesca were unable to explore **Umbertown**, the name for the tiefling underground, further because the bouncer had been murdered by a mysterious pair of masquerade mask wearing people who killed with poison and fled the brothel\n    72→\n    73→- The party secured the help of a few **Farmer's Daughters**, local farmers turned mercenaries who's mammoths were able to traverse the everstorm surrounding Windhaven, however when they met up with their caravan at the north gate, they uncovered one of Nuum's shipments of fish ... upon further investigation of their goods, they found the latest shipment of children\n    74→\n    75→  - Dispatching the guards who turned into monstrous snake beings, the party entrusted the safety of the few orphaned children (**Reela, Rory and Squat)** that they managed to save to the Farmer's Daughters as they had more pressing matters at Cloud City\n    76→\n    77→- The path to the \"Second Eye\" of the everstorm outside of town was paved with the bodies of numerous orcs and humans who had died in combat, there was a strange thick black ooze which coated the fallen, none of their weapons and armor could be found\n    78→\n    79→  - There was a large red dragon who could be seen above the cloud cover, it seemed to be attacking a caravan in the distance, however the party attempted to keep a low profile.\n    80→\n    81→- After they looked to have been spotted by the dragon, the party was \"rescued\" after stumbling into a tranquil circle of autumn trees, in its center on a tree stump, stood **Reynaldine of the Autumn Court**, a creature whose true origin was recently uncovered by Nyx to be an abomination of 4 entities with deep regret for his own existence.\n    82→\n    83→  - He told the party that he had been collecting the Elemental Seals to the Harvest's Zenith and that he intended to \"Fix Everything\" with them, Nyx who's connection with this entity goes deeper than purely the power he supplies as part of his patronage decided to help him... it was that or deal with the clearly overmatched FeyLord and the Dracolich who gave him a lift here: **Rimefang**\n    84→\n    85→  - **Kelik Temptus, Prince of the Air Genasi and Keeper of the Seal of Temptus** died that day when he was absorbed back into his amulet upon it being handed over to Reynaldine\n    86→\n    87→  - Claiming to have already collected the Fire and Earth seals, Reynaldine tasked the party with retrieving the water amulet which was on **Lapis Poseidon**, the water genasi princess who hung in a glass prison within Kelik's Father's throne-room\n    88→\n    89→  - During the fight, Brigit discovered that her stag form resembled the real Stag Zodiac, **Shi Shi Gami**, who's antlers formed the locket that is displayed across her forehead\n    90→\n    91→- The party was then left in the Everstorm without their guides still tasked with heading to Cloud City, however this time to retrieve the last seal to the Harvest's Zenith\n    92→\n    93→  - Thanks to Brigit's newfound Air Elemental Power, the party was able to fly above the storm and make it to the Second Eye, but were attacked by some Giants and their pet Roc's\n    94→\n    95→  - The party fled through a lightning struck birch tree after activating an ancient network of trees which seem to interlock every major city in Mardov\n    96→\n    97→- The party made their way to **The Roost Hotel and Casino** where they were greeted by the Penguin Aarakocra, **Mr. Tux**, who informed the party of the fiends who are assaulting **Euclid's Study** within the **Guest Services** portion of the hotel\n    98→\n    99→  - Brigit, Fen and Varys went directly to the **Temple of the 9 Winds** where the throneroom of **Terrell Temptus**, **the Storm King**. They learned that he had already discovered that there was a plot to kidnap the water genasi princess and they were tasked with rooting out the culprit and bringing them to the king, they too headed back to join the others at the Roost with a royal letter, granting them the Royal Suite at the top of the hotel... for free!\n   100→\n   101→  - The others headed to the **Ice-House** to exchange their gold for the local form of currency known as **Electrum**, there they met a Bronze Dragon named **Zepheeron** who took a liking to Sprocket's \"generocity\"\n   102→\n   103→    - They learned the **Zepheeron** is the guardian of a strangely shaped building known as **The Hanging Gardens** however he also lends his lightning to the device which creates Electrum two gold pieces are fused with the lightning and a combination of Cirrus and Nimbus bricks\n   104→\n   105→    - The party passed the **Hanging Gardens** in **Nimbus Town**, the temple is noteworthy as it is in the shape of two pyramids stacked at their square bases, the temple looks to be in three parts, a center and two pyramid tops and bottoms. The striped pattern has lavish vines and other foliage that drape its sides partially filling in the gaps left in the building.\n   106→\n   107→- Back at the Roost, Randal was playing for free room and board (not knowing that the others already secured it) when he was surrounded by agents of **Lorelai Clary** who proceeded to sabotage his performance and capture him, they were dodged thanks to his friends coming to the rescue but **Fen Darkeyes** was taken in his stead by one of the two twin Succubus/Incubus named **Minnie LaDoux**\n   108→\n   109→  - He was taken down to Guest Services and was given a command: \"Head to the Throneroom when the Jack Falls and Retrieve the Water Genasi Princess\"\n   110→\n   111→  - The agents of Lorelai's crew turned out to be Hewie, Dewie, and Louie of the travelling siegeball team. Additionally there was a Crane Aarakocra who has yet to be unmasked... (the elder oobliex)\n   112→\n   113→- The Party captured Minnie's brother **Goadie LaDoux**, manager of the Casino when they found him eavesdropping on their room, they impersonated him to get close to Lorelai within the Casino however their meeting turned violent fast:\n   114→\n   115→  - The fighting was over quick when it became apparent that the party was much more experienced in combat than their opponents\n   116→\n   117→  - During the fight, the party had them on the ropes and nearly killed Lorelai\n   118→\n   119→  - Things turned sour when the mother of all distractions occurred .... Jack Fell!\n   120→\n   121→  - Chaos ensued, most of the Red Skipper were handed over to the authories, but the Crane just barely slipped through the grasp of the party\n   122→\n   123→  - Fen had a compulsion activated on him which caused him to also slip away from the others in the following confusion\n   124→\n   125→- In the aftermath of the party:\n   126→\n   127→  - Randal Legend-Lord **Reynaldine of the Autumn Court** and learned that:\n   128→\n   129→    - he is actually 4 beings in one, who we now know the names of:\n   130→\n   131→      - **Juliannia of the Summer Court,** Archfey and Nyx's Patron\n   132→\n   133→      - **Grazz't, The Demon Prince of Pleasure**\n   134→\n   135→      - **Gabriel the Kind,** Archangel\n   136→\n   137→      - **Reynardine. Prince of Harvest**, Archfey who is currently housing the other entities within him\n   138→\n   139→    - The Primal Elemental Evils were Sealed by the 4 archFey before the incident which consumed Juliannia, from the seals the genasi leaked out\n   140→\n   141→    - Tiamat was broken into 5 pieces by Bahamut who was being followed by the Archangels, Gabriel included.... These 3 angels were gifted a seal of their own. The purpose of these seals is unknown\n   142→\n   143→  - Nyx and Stalwart interrogated Lorelai who ended up touching her broach \\<which is magic in its own right\\> and transfer the effects of a dream spell which allowed the two long lost sisters to finally communicate! Nyx finally got to meet **Xyn Khamestria**, her younger sister who now looks the same age\n   144→\n   145→    - Stalwart during this time was seen single handedly wrestling a the owlbear, **Princess**, down to the ground to calm it down during the drone of the mixed gusts of wind that now thrashed about all of Cloud City\n   146→\n   147→    - Nyx learned that:\n   148→\n   149→      - Xyn was aged forward thanks to her interaction with Leonidas earlier in her own story, it is uncertain what caused this event.\n   150→\n   151→      - She believes the key to opening the Harvest's Zenith is written within a book which was originally stolen from **Euclid** by Reynaldine: **The Origin of Genasi *: and The Rebirth of the Progenitors***\n   152→\n   153→      - Xyn has **Gabriel the Kind** as her patron, he lives in the teddy bear that she calls **Mr. Happy**. Making her the second Khamestria to be a patron of the 4 part entity known as Reyanldine\n   154→\n   155→      - The Mountain's Zenith was opened and that let out **Barachiel the Righteous** who had a portion of his power locked within, sparking a partial rebirth within the Shadowfell.\n   156→\n   157→      - Mr. Happy is a small vessel for Gabriel's power, Xyn believes if she can make it to the Shadowfell, that vessel's potential will grow but she knows that if the Havest's Zenith is opened that Gabriel will also be reborn in part.\n   158→\n   159→      - Gabriel is needed in the Shadowfell to quell the fighting between his brothers. \"Without Kindness, there is no way to align Justice and Righteousness\"\n   160→\n   161→      - Xyn is in Cloud City to get ahold of the water amulet so that Reynaldine will open the Havest's Zenith, though Xyn vows to try and lock him out with Gabriel's help\n   162→\n   163→      - Shes not alone, there are multiple factions trying to get ahold of the entity\n   164→\n   165→        - Fire Genasi are angry and headed towards that area\n   166→\n   167→        - Serpents within the water's apparatus are trying to revive an ancient serpent to eat the world\n   168→\n   169→      - Euclid's Study is being assaulted and will not hold for long, they are looking for the Origin of Genasi book.... Of which Xyn has the only copy\n   170→\n   171→  - Fen had a longer adventure where he ended up being framed for the murder of King Temptus and the kidnapping of Lapis, however to escape a truely unfortunate fate he envoked Reynaldine's power, blacking out and waking up on the way back from Guest Services.. Empty handed\n   172→\n   173→- The others re-gathered their party and attempted to stop Cloud City from plummeting into the city below it...\n   174→\n   175→- Tesca leads the reunited party through an urban sprawl to the **Elamier's Flask**, a potion shop in Nimbus town which has a special potion that can replicate any item it touches, however it comes at a great cost. Lorelai had a jar of it set aside for herself and was happy to offer her help in exchange for more ... amenable company\n   176→\n   177→  - Their plan is to use it to repair Jack by lathering its center in the potion\n   178→\n   179→- The party bumps into the bronze dragon that Fen recognizes from his solo adventure... behind enemy lines. Sprocket and Stalwart recognize the dragon from their trip to the bank ... after all the dragon ended up being the one to mint the electrum when surrounded by 12 air genasi all channeling their power\n   180→\n   181→  - **Zepheeron the Bronze** tells the party that he will head to the Apparatus within the Hanging Gardens to help keep Cloud City afloat in the absence of The Storm King or Seal of Temptus...\n   182→\n   183→  - This is the same apparatus that the Storm King referenced, allowed him to scry on Kelik and drag the party into this whole mess\n   184→\n   185→- The party finds 3 factions fighting at Jackfall Landing: The Air Genasi, Attacking Orcs and The Giants who seemingly betrayed their former comrades, the party fought back wave after wave of orcs and after a long seemingly unending fight were finally rescued by **The Thornberry Wilde**, a sky whale ship helmed by **Captain Liza** with her rag-tag crew\n   186→\n   187→  - Notably the orcs are wearing a sigil which denotes Faeish, a village that Brigit grew up near\n   188→\n   189→- Once aboard they witnessed a red dragon and fire genasi on his back attacking the Hanging Garden, specifically a metallic object which is now orbiting the temple.\n   190→\n   191→  - Over the course of the fight, they work out that this is **Blaine Cowen,** Fire Genasi Royal Guard and **Volcanis, the Red**, they are wrestling power away from the Bronze Dragon, they were originally searching for the fire genasi princess, **Ember Pyra** but she was taken by fiends. He head here to scry for her and found this\n   192→\n   193→  - Sprocket touches the satellite and discovers that its currently scrying on a hanging city ... upside-down hanging city with a dual spheroid apparatus on the particular platform within that city that the vision scoped in on behind the platform is a similarly dual pyramid shape with 7 floors all rotating out of phase with each other horizontally ... the Harvest's Zenith!\n   194→\n   195→  - The fight ends and the two sides speak, specifically Blaine comments on the familial connection between **Xyn and Nyx**\n   196→\n   197→"}]},"uuid":"5b54d3a3-083e-40dc-bc78-73cf5b3432aa","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:45.191Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3d214ae2-3f64-44fe-a679-07cbff05b260","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"a5d6acc1-22c2-4f6d-9918-16cc3caa2f80","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"callback"},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_01Xf9DimuzMn9yLzqMZtTPjU","toolUseID":"toolu_01Xf9DimuzMn9yLzqMZtTPjU","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:46.085Z","uuid":"97432d3d-f956-4541-801d-865dc3c7af3b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
{"parentUuid":"8e1ae66f-25bb-4b4b-8996-ec882391687a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Xf9DimuzMn9yLzqMZtTPjU","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: location\n     3→category: city\n     4→name: WindHaven\n     5→parent: \"[[DawnHaven]]\"\n     6→aliases:\n     7→  - Windhaven\n     8→notable_npcs:\n     9→  - \"[[Leonidas]]\"\n    10→  - \"[[Cesar Bordesia]]\"\n    11→  - \"[[Kite Curden]]\"\n    12→  - \"[[Lady Sessura Vaught]]\"\n    13→  - \"[[Mathia Nuum]]\"\n    14→chapters:\n    15→  - Act 1\n    16→  - Act 2\n    17→source: Places/DawnHaven/WindHaven/\n    18→maps:\n    19→  - \"[[WindHaven.jpg]]\"\n    20→tags:\n    21→  - city\n    22→  - windhaven\n    23→  - dawnhaven\n    24→  - harvest-festival\n    25→---\n    26→\n    27→# WindHaven\n    28→\n    29→A walled city of windmills and farmland in the heart of [[DawnHaven]], dominated by the colossal **Windmill Willow** -- an ancient sentient tree whose massive pink-blossomed canopy towers over the entire settlement. WindHaven is ruled by the Androsphinx [[Leonidas]], styled \"Leader of Summer's Last Light,\" who resides within the Willow. The city serves as a major campaign hub where the party first gathers and where the Harvest Festival triggers catastrophic events.\n    30→\n    31→## Geography & Architecture\n    32→\n    33→### Landscape\n    34→The city map shows WindHaven as a walled settlement on a coastal bluff. The enormous Windmill Willow dominates the center-north, its cherry-blossom canopy spreading over half the city. Multiple windmills dot the landscape both within and outside the walls. To the west, sea cliffs drop to a rocky shoreline with a grotto below. Green farmland stretches south and east, dotted with fringe farms along the Game Trail. Rolling hills and scattered trees surround the settlement.\n    35→\n    36→### Architectural Style\n    37→Medieval European with a pastoral character. Stone walls and timber-frame buildings, many with thatched roofs. The city is tiered, with the Upper Crust (nobility) on higher ground and common districts below. The Windmill Willow itself functions as architecture -- its trunk houses the library, lobby, and Leonidas's chambers, while its branches support platforms. Windmills are both functional (grinding grain) and cultural symbols -- they appear on the city's sigil.\n    38→\n    39→### Color Palette\n    40→Dominated by the Willow's explosion of pink cherry blossoms against the green of farmland and grey-brown of stone walls. Warm golden-brown timber, green copper rooftops, and the blue-grey of the coastal waters. The city sigil renders in dark iron tones.\n    41→\n    42→### Lighting & Atmosphere\n    43→Pastoral and warm. The reference art emphasizes golden sunlight filtering through blossom petals. The Windmill Willow painting shows a face-like formation in the ancient bark, with dappled green-gold light -- the tree is wise, watchful, alive. During the Harvest Festival, the atmosphere shifts to twilight purple and festival-fire orange, culminating in the demonic red of the Night of the Equinox.\n    44→\n    45→### Scale & Density\n    46→Medium-sized city. Dense within walls, scattered farms outside. The Windmill Willow is impossibly large -- its canopy covers hundreds of meters.\n    47→\n    48→## Districts\n    49→\n    50→### City Proper\n    51→The walled inner city, organized around the Windmill Willow.\n    52→\n    53→**Upper Crust** -- Nobility district. Home to [[Cesar Bordesia|Count Cesar Bordesia]], the secretly Yuan-Ti [[Mathia Nuum]], and the Windswept Orphanage. Contains The Rookery.\n    54→\n    55→**North Gate** -- Main entrance district with the North Post trading post, guards, and shoppers. Second Windswept Orphanage location here.\n    56→\n    57→**Temples** -- Religious quarter containing:\n    58→- [[Avandra's Gift]] -- Temple to the goddess of travel\n    59→- [[Bahamut Makeshift Tent Temple]] -- Temporary temple\n    60→- [[Field of Pelor]] -- Temple to the sun god\n    61→- Melora Sanctum portal\n    62→\n    63→**Shops** -- Commercial district with Claire Yeendle's pawn shop, Durst Nam's Zoomybrew, Kiersten Vettlegreen's weapons, Melbrum Gam's armor shop, and Oliver Hyrdantos's Willowtop Library.\n    64→\n    65→**Windmill Willow** -- The living heart of the city. Multiple levels within the ancient tree: main lobby, library, Leonidas's chambers, and a summit lookout.\n    66→\n    67→### Brothels, Inns & Taverns\n    68→\n    69→**Traveler's Pony** -- Split establishment with an Elf Half and Human Half, plus Umbertown below. Run by Alice. Features the Orchid Cloaked bard dancer [[Mina Sultrin]], the [[Scarlet Tunic Brigade]], and [[Sarah Pudnam]]'s Farmer's Daughters.\n    70→\n    71→### The Game Trail\n    72→The wilderness road leading out of WindHaven, containing:\n    73→\n    74→**Fringe Farms:**\n    75→- [[Fallmora Commune]] -- Fringe farm\n    76→- [[Fellfall Farms]] -- Working farm\n    77→- [[Navakarra Steading]] -- Family farm with cemetery (Annabelle & Mankato Navakarra)\n    78→- [[Serenity's Passing]] -- Farm near WindHaven\n    79→- [[Vespeera Ranch]] -- Ranch run by Cauldera Freewind\n    80→- [[Wesrun Field]] -- Crow-haunted field with family crypt and battlemap\n    81→\n    82→**Path To the Second Eye:**\n    83→- Attack of the Fire Genasi encounter\n    84→- Caravan encounters (Farmer's Daughters, Lord Godbrand Fartheshire's caravan)\n    85→- Shipment Bearing Lord Nuum's Sigil / Slavers encounter\n    86→- [[Skyfall Cemetery]] -- Graveyard with fortune teller No Mi\n    87→- The Second Eye -- Canyon with giants\n    88→- Tower Bridge Battalion\n    89→\n    90→**North Cliffs:**\n    91→- Razelsin Shortcut -- Entrance to the [[feywilde]] via the Four Corner Door\n    92→\n    93→### The Grotto\n    94→Coastal cave system below the city. Contains Doldrums Lighthouse, Siren's Cove, and Windsear Bluff (Goddess Path).\n    95→\n    96→### SchroomTop\n    97→Mushroom settlement near WindHaven. Features Chanterelle, Tinker's Bench, and views from the Rice Flats.\n    98→\n    99→### Harvest Festival\n   100→Annual celebration that serves as a major campaign turning point.\n   101→\n   102→**Hour of Twilight** -- Evening festivities beneath the Windmill Willow's shadow, convergence scene.\n   103→\n   104→**The Night of the Equinox** -- Catastrophic event. Features the Ascended Tygras, Harvest Mother, Harvesters, Reynaldine's true form, and the Shadow of Famine. Gate guardians manifest.\n   105→\n   106→## Key NPCs\n   107→\n   108→- **[[Leonidas]]** -- Androsphinx ruler, \"Leader of Summer's Last Light\"\n   109→- **[[Kite Curden]]** -- Guard Captain\n   110→- **[[Cesar Bordesia]]** -- Count of WindHaven (Upper Crust)\n   111→- **[[Lady Sessura Vaught]]** -- Secret leader of the [[Scales of Falak]] in WindHaven\n   112→- **[[Mathia Nuum]]** -- Noblewoman, secretly a Yuan-Ti\n   113→- **[[Themballa Illish]]** -- Servant of the Sphinx\n   114→\n   115→## History\n   116→\n   117→WindHaven is where the party assembles for the first time, drawn by various threads to the city during the Harvest Festival. The festival culminates in the Night of the Equinox -- a catastrophic event where [[Reynaldine]] (Fey Lord of Famine) manifests, the Shadow of Famine descends, and the Harvest's Zenith connection is revealed. This event drives the party out of WindHaven and into the wider campaign.\n   118→\n   119→The city's connection to the [[Scales of Falak]] (Yuan-Ti cult) runs deep -- [[Lady Sessura Vaught]] operates openly in high society, and the secretly Yuan-Ti [[Mathia Nuum]] holds noble status.\n   120→\n   121→## Gallery\n   122→\n   123→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/WindHaven.jpg]]\n   124→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/Sigil of WindHaven.png]]\n   125→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/City Proper/Windmill Willow/Windmill Willow.jpeg]]\n   126→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/City Proper/Windmill Willow/Summer's Last Light.jpg]]\n   127→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/City Proper/Windmill Willow/Library within the Willow.jpg]]\n   128→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/Harvest Festival/Hour of Twilight/Hour of Twilight.jpg]]\n   129→![[assets/maps-source/DawnHaven/WindHaven/Harvest Festival/The Night of the Equinox/The Equinox.jpg]]\n   130→"}]},"uuid":"250fb244-55b0-4048-bc15-828b55746a0d","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:46.084Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8e1ae66f-25bb-4b4b-8996-ec882391687a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"b9a7b0a8-7e49-4dcc-9bd4-05272d388a93","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"progress","data":{"type":"hook_progress","hookEvent":"PostToolUse","hookName":"PostToolUse:Read","command":"\"$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR/.claude/hooks/check-discord-queue.sh\""},"parentToolUseID":"toolu_011qBskXcsSWKt2hZSSC53ns","toolUseID":"toolu_011qBskXcsSWKt2hZSSC53ns","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:46.951Z","uuid":"009c6409-23de-4ed2-8ed8-c3fe5147457b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"b9a7b0a8-7e49-4dcc-9bd4-05272d388a93","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011qBskXcsSWKt2hZSSC53ns","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Euclid\"\n     4→source: [\"World Anvil\"]\n     5→world_anvil_category: \"\"\n     6→---\n     7→\n     8→# Euclid\n     9→\n    10→An Owl Aarakocra Wizard who works closely with the Owl Zodiac, Archimedes. A very old individual who has been around since the creation of the Celestial Realm. He got his start as a fledgling archivist working under Gabriel the Kind and worked his way up the ladder to become Gabriel's Keeper of the Divine Seal.\n    11→He did so under the condition that he would be safe from Fiends and so he was taught a Forbiddence Spell to keep him safe. Prior to the Collapse of the 7 Heavens, Euclid was approached by a younger and curious Reynardine of the Autumn Court, whom he saw a spark within and agreed to let the Fey Prince study magic under him. Together they spent countless years learning magic and researching the Progenitors. A book of note that the two of them had an argument over was the Origin of Genasi, a tome which indicated the power of the Elemental Evils and the emergence of the Genasi who rose from the ashes of the calamity they caused. During this time, Reynardine would spend large amounts of time out on missions to recover artifacts for Euclid, including donating his own pan-flute which he traded Euclid for safe keeping. In thanks, he offered the Aarakocra a refuge among his people once he assumed his rightful place as FeyLorde of the Autumn Court. \"I have entrusted you a power greater than the moon, and in payment for your help guarding it, you will be granted from me a favor and the moon\"\n    12→Euclid's Library on the moon was named Alexandria, though he would not take up residence there for some time, the library was tethered to the aarakocra's ancestral home on an isle of clouds, Laputa Town. When the 7 Heaven's fell, Euclid retreated back to Alexandria and entrusted Archimedes to transfer the Forbiddence Spell over to their new refuge. During the Elemental Wars that followed, Euclid made an agreement with the old Air Genasi General and follower of the fallen Reynardine that he would allow Laputa Town to be anchored to the newly formed Cloud City under the condition that he be allowed to modify the Forbiddence Spell into a contract that could only be undone by The Ragnarok Protocol. The new Forbiddence Spell would now apply to Reynaldine of the Autumn Court, the being who now inhabited the once spirited Fey Prince. That would apply to the entirety of Cloud City which would be allowed to use the Immovable Rod central to Laputa Town as a contingency plan in case the king were to die. This agreement occurred as a coalition between the Storm King Terrell Temptus, Euclid, and\n    13→Leonidas of Summer's Last Light\n    14→. All three having reached a peace treaty at the end of the conflict. The Fire Genasi agreed to hand over the Water Genasi princess, Windhaven's Harvest Festival was created as a tithe to the Spring Court, and Leonidas was granted a sanctum which would preserve Juliannia's last moments forever.\n    15→Recently, the Ragnarok Protocol was activated, removing the Forbiddence Spell from Alexandria, Cloud City and WindHaven. Stalwart, Nyx and Brigit (using her Owl Zodiac Form) were able to re-activate the spell, however Xyn with her patron\n    16→Mr. Happy\n    17→stayed behind to finish charging the spell.\n    18→"}]},"uuid":"c3ba401b-a349-4a61-ac6c-9c1015db694b","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:46.950Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b9a7b0a8-7e49-4dcc-9bd4-05272d388a93","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"68262a2b-45a2-4071-ae6c-9ee8a026faa3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KFKNLiWYwsjyrYxx2ReVSq","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: faction\n     3→name: \"Fey Courts\"\n     4→aliases: [\"The Four Courts\", \"Courts of the Feywild\"]\n     5→category: planar\n     6→leader: \"Various Fey Lords\"\n     7→headquarters: \"[[Feywild]]\"\n     8→status: active\n     9→source: [\"intrigue-web.md\", \"world-bible.md\", \"npcs-catalog.md\"]\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→# The Fey Courts\n    13→\n    14→## Overview\n    15→\n    16→The [[Feywild|Fae Wildes]] are organized into four seasonal courts, each with its own domain and leadership. The courts are deeply entangled with the campaign's politics through [[Reynaldine]]'s machinations and the Convergence event.\n    17→\n    18→## The Four Courts\n    19→\n    20→### Spring Court — Brier Patch\n    21→- **Fey Lord:** Evylanndrin of the Spring Court\n    22→- **Eladrin Representative:** [[Haden Dar]] (Platinum Seat of Illusionist Covenant)\n    23→\n    24→### Summer Court — Flowerfield\n    25→- **Fey Lord:** Juliannia of the Summer Court\n    26→- **Eladrin Representative:** Jing Lin (Voice of Potential)\n    27→\n    28→### Autumn Court — Reaper's Bounty\n    29→- **Fey Lord:** [[Reynaldine]] of the Autumn Court\n    30→- **Role in Campaign:** The most politically active court. [[Reynaldine]] serves [[Vecna]] while pursuing his own agenda — to ascend to godhood and save Gabriel (archangel of kindness).\n    31→- **Key Actions:**\n    32→  - Hired Annie Hallowbrook to deliver [[Kelik Waystar]] to [[windhaven]]\n    33→  - Used [[Xyn]] (Nyx's twin sister) as messenger\n    34→  - Threatened to disrupt pestilence shipments to [[rimefang]] as leverage\n    35→  - Invoked the Ritual of the Stone Table through Umbara Skuttle\n    36→  - True plan revealed Ch. 133 — orchestrated everything for his own ascension\n    37→  - \"Crippled\" Ch. 106; cloak fell revealing leeches forming a portal\n    38→- **Queen of Thorns** — Referenced Autumn Court figure\n    39→\n    40→### Winter Court — Snowcrest Sanctum\n    41→- **Known Members:**\n    42→  - Grumblesin of the Winter Court\n    43→  - Silence (Timothy of the Feywild) — Therzadun's vessel\n    44→  - Tallow (Squire of Winter)\n    45→  - Yeth Hounds\n    46→- **Eladrin Representative:** Tumnus Driftwalker (Nic of the [[Winter's Curators]])\n    47→\n    48→> **CRITICAL NOTE:** [[Reynaldine]] (Fey Lord of the Autumn Court) is a DISTINCT entity from [[Reynardine]] (Nyx's patron, \"the Madness Bringer\"). Similar names, different characters.\n    49→\n    50→## The Convergence (Late Campaign)\n    51→\n    52→All planes began converging into the Feywild:\n    53→- Orchestrated by [[Reynaldine]]\n    54→- **Stone Table Ritual:** sacrifice + benefactor = sovereignty\n    55→- Knights needed: [[Riven Fen|Fen]], [[Josh Coolbreeze|Josh]], [[Varis Liadon|Varis]], [[Sprocket Overspark|Sprocket]]\n    56→- [[Graz'zt]] swapped with Gabriel at the ritual\n    57→- [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] forced both himself and [[Graz'zt]] onto the Stone Table\n    58→- **Result:** [[Bergs Fireforge|Bergs]] becomes deity of Mardov's Feywild\n    59→- Shattering of Stone Table shatters the Feywild and Mardov\n    60→\n    61→## Allies & Enemies\n    62→\n    63→### Allies\n    64→- The party (sometimes, through [[Reynaldine]]'s triple-agent scheme)\n    65→- [[lolth]] (made deal with [[Raven Queen]] against [[Asmodeus]])\n    66→\n    67→### Enemies\n    68→- [[Vecna]] (controls [[Reynaldine]] — \"I OWN YOU\")\n    69→- [[Asmodeus]] (killed Ch. 136)\n    70→"}]},"uuid":"58438b3f-e376-4ee5-9d7c-8324bff9acd5","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:58:47.280Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"68262a2b-45a2-4071-ae6c-9ee8a026faa3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
