D. Wells to discover more about the author of a book - Freld Poxtram. A fairytale centaur. Apparently he knows how to make wine. Hooray!\n    12→\n    13→Fen looks for a book on brewing - a human book with some gnomish writing on it that discussing brewing with Roots - A ROOT BEER\n    14→\n    15→Hidleson Green of Shroomtop\n    16→\n    17→Sprocket and Fen head to the market to buy ingredients for sample alcohol to sell\n    18→\n    19→Windhaven has changed a bit. There's so many wares now to be sold at the harvest festival. But there's still drab all around, the city seems to have acclimated to their presence though\n    20→\n    21→Brigit pushes a drab into an alley semi-steakily to search the drab's body for a spell written on his skin.\n    22→\n    23→Last name - Hilbrant. Part of a family. There is a tattoo underneath his beard of a sun. And a sun pendant, which no one has taken. Brigit takes said pendant.\n    24→\n    25→Fen, Sprocket and Brigit go shopping for brewery ingredients and Brigit buys some diamonds and Sprockets sells a giant opal\n    26→\n    27→And if we get a chance to swing by the Magister's Circle, Eon, the alabaster seat isn't doing well\n    28→\n    29→There's going to be an election process to pick the next one.\n    30→\n    31→The Emerald Veil\n    32→\n    33→Randall casts Legend Lore on Summer and is creating a narrative performance.\n    34→\n    35→He's reminded of a still statuesque situtaion that happened at the top of the Willow, last summer's night. Leonidas has the creation of the death of Julianna who is being transformed into the quartet, the abomination itself.\n    36→\n    37→The starcrossed lovers - the summer queen being betrothed to autumn is scorned when he cheats on her and she is then taken by Gratz.\n    38→\n    39→The summer queen was beautiful before her fate turned. Followed by marigolds wherever she walked. She still exists to this day. Just avoid the tale of the demon prince of pleasure.\n    40→\n    41→So Randall is going to make a song of hope - Marigold Wine.\n    42→\n    43→Varis is chilling in the bar\n    44→\n    45→Varis meets a beige ninja. He wants to trade a tale for a bottle of wine. Matthew Windsmith\n    46→\n    47→He double checks to see if this is Reynaldine, but it's not.\n    48→\n    49→But he's definitely not human either.\n    50→\n    51→It's an air genasi.\n    52→\n    53→An elven woman, drunken, heads up to Varis and includes him in the conversation. Asks to tell him a tale. She's from Tower Bridge! He would tell her a story of escape - escape from another world.\n    54→\n    55→And finally, Sam, she doesn't feel safe, there's not enough trees, not enough places to hide. And he suggests that she could maybe go to the Druid's Grove in Var'Taos.\n    56→\n    57→OOOH she knows Yvonne\n    58→\n    59→Finally, he talks to the bouncer. Itori, from Monethern. She didn't want to live in her mother's shadow, in her mother's expectations.\n    60→\n    61→She's from the Veritasia Palace.\n    62→\n    63→Randall lands\n    64→\n    65→Heads to the inn\n    66→\n    67→REAL QUICK - Brigit flies up and tries to locate the seal 3 times every 2000 ft, doesn't find it anywhere\n    68→\n    69→REAL QUICK - Fen gets a ride to Cloud City early tomorrow - Sun's first light, but he'll get back by sundown. The Storm Peaks.\n    70→\n    71→To the tavern\n    72→\n    73→Randall is greeted by some the Umbertown Kaval of Horns in the tavern - they wish to hear another tale from him, just not about a pegasus\n    74→\n    75→Randall also lets all of us know about the marigold wine. MARKETING\n    76→\n    77→Randall performs the 2 doctors. And ensures that Sprocket is aware that this song is about him and Stalwart.\n    78→\n    79→Sprocket has no idea. But Randall can't tell if it's because he's distracted or not. Brigit recognizes and starts crying.\n    80→\n    81→Whichever doctor the crowd responds to best - the tall doctor - drinks a glass of marigold wine.\n    82→\n    83→NAT 20 TIPS\n    84→\n    85→He discusses the performance with a fellow bard - who says that dancing is all the rage nowadays.\n    86→\n    87→Upstairs\n    88→\n    89→We discuss the alcohol\n    90→\n    91→We're going to make a marigold wine and a orange, apricot beer\n    92→\n    93→Brigit takes cuttings from the ingredients to make labels tomorrow.\n    94→\n    95→SKILL CHALLENGE\n    96→\n    97→Sprocket runs the shots and lets everyone know what to do at the exact times\n    98→\n    99→Fen is smashing up the ingredients and chopping\n   100→\n   101→Varis taste tests along the way and ensures we don't poison ourselves or burn the inn down\n   102→\n   103→BUT HE FAILS. He's been drinking too much today. We have one less bottle of wine.\n   104→\n   105→Brigit and Randall and some eagles and the Pegasus take the beer up to the stratosphere to cool the alcohol\n   106→\n   107→Overall success, we have 5 bottles of wine, 6 bottles of beer\n   108→\n   109→Flying down, we see a griffin land.\n   110→\n   111→He wants to meet Randall for a joust tomorrow at sundown.\n   112→\n   113→Varis, Brigit and Randall go to bed\n   114→\n   115→Brigit makes vines and flowers on the bottles, but she's a little drunk, so tomorrow.\n   116→\n   117→Harvest Day IPA\n   118→\n   119→The Return of Summer Marigold Wine\n   120→\n   121→BUT WAIT we need to age the wine\n   122→\n   123→Fen and Sprocket head out\n   124→\n   125→Fen and Sprocket's Adventure\n   126→\n   127→The town has changed in preparation - a paper lantern display\n   128→\n   129→The librarian is of course inside the library\n   130→\n   131→But be careful to not mention her to Leonidas as it is almost the anniversary of her death.\n   132→\n   133→Sprocket is able to turn his third eye to Sylvan, he sees flowerbuds in the crack of the hinge. The writing says \"So long as last summer's light exists, I shall stay for you my queen.\"\n   134→\n   135→Sprocket and Fen see it's cracking and he's able to walk through the door\n   136→\n   137→The sun has set in his sanctum\n   138→\n   139→Sprocket pushes Fen forward\n   140→\n   141→Leonidas is not okay. He's sad.\n   142→\n   143→What's wrong - It's over, he's won. And now assassins have killed Spring. The Feywilde is lost, there will be no festival tomorrow.\n   144→\n   145→There's tea party tomorrow - we shouldn't miss it maybe?\n   146→\n   147→Leonidas shames Fen for not visiting his brother in Monethern after saving him.\n   148→\n   149→HE'S GONNA DO IT\n   150→\n   151→The abomination will be undone and the evil within will run free.\n   152→\n   153→Fuck\n   154→\n   155→He's in a jail. Tomorrow's events will free him though\n   156→\n   157→Two days from now will be his victory day, as it has happened before. Windhaven will not last - it was founded on a promise.\n   158→\n   159→So then. What's next?\n   160→\n   161→Leonidas only knows through tomorrow, because tomorrow he dies\n   162→\n   163→He saw a timeline change once - in the trial of Fen.\n   164→\n   165→His sister lies dormant in the sunless citadel\n   166→\n   167→He's never seen past tomorrow. It is destiny.\n   168→\n   169→It's too late for anything now, but he'll see us again sometime. He owes some friends of theirs a favor.\n   170→\n   171→Fen stays for a bit and tries to cheer him up with tales of Evelon.\n   172→\n   173→They take the Return of Summer and drink 3 glasses - to the inevitable\n   174→\n   175→They head down\n   176→\n   177→Stalwart lets it slip that the Festival might not be happening. And someone is going to escape from jail.\n   178→\n   179→The guard is having a rough time\n   180→\n   181→TO SLEEP\n   182→\n   183→TO DAY\n   184→\n   185→Randall tries to get some information from his followers on the Queen of Thorns\n   186→\n   187→He gets back 3 rumors - 2 false, one true.\n   188→\n   189→(1) The queen of thorns has a band of emerald cloak bards who have taken up residence and will be at the tea party\n   190→\n   191→(2) She has a pet white rabbit who is always late\n   192→\n   193→(3) She loves the color red\n   194→\n   195→Brigit heads to the glass etchers\n   196→\n   197→Commune with Nature - there's a ton of caves and natural stuff underneath.\n   198→\n   199→Powerful beings - there's been quite movement here. Pretty powerful fey here. And the nature felt Leonidas for once - he's leaving. The fiends are that bad. But there's a shitton of celestials down below windmill willow. 200+\n   200→\n   201→Influence from other planes of existence - someone else opened up the windmill willow and walk through it. They were kind of moldy. And it felt like death.\n   202→\n   203→Brigit meets two teenagers- Kima and Francois. Greater restores the drab dad, the brewer of the beer books.\n   204→\n   205→He's back for 10 seconds. They understandably freak and are so happy.\n   206→\n   207→Brigit can only do this once a day, but she's going to try to come back tomorrow.\n   208→\n   209→She wishes she could do more, but in the meantime assures them they're doing all they can taking care of their dad. They give her root to immediately age alcohol.\n   210→\n   211→Bottles etched for free for good deeds. Hoorah.\n   212→\n   213→Varis and Randall head to Westrun fields and there are murders and murders of crows. They look like scarecrows from the sky, but something is weird.\n   214→\n   215→Varis is basically invisible. He sees a little girl carrying a pail of flowers.\n   216→\n   217→And yes, these are zombies.\n   218→\n   219→Her dog sniffs Varis out though - a giant husky, Balto.\n   220→\n   221→Aeris Westrun. She picks flowers for the market.\n   222→\n   223→Alright yeah, so this is actually her family.\n   224→\n   225→The flowers stopped growing after her great grandpa tried to grow flowers with the same bouquet of flowers grown from the same soil, so now they grow flowers in people/zombies whatever.\n   226→\n   227→Randall heads down to help talk to the kid.\n   228→\n   229→These flowers are unnaturally grown; from a magical sense\n   230→\n   231→She is alone except for Balto.\n   232→\n   233→THERE'S A GUY THAT GUYS FLOWERS, ONE FOR EACH FINGER AND HE BUYS 6 EVERY TIME\n   234→\n   235→MOTHERFUCKER\n   236→\n   237→He'll be back in 2 weeks. He comes by every 6 weeks.\n   238→\n   239→Varis hands her 50 gold and has her put the 6-fingered man on a blacklist.\n   240→\n   241→And weirdly, Lorelei owns this estate, Randall's employee\n   242→\n   243→Varis takes a look at one of the zombies - there's a gravestone. Barnibus Westrun and his dog spot. Most have their dogs buried beneath them.\n   244→\n   245→Varis and Randall head to the masoleum\n   246→\n   247→No undead, but it still includes a man and his dog. Jesepth Westrun and Winklebottom. With a Jester's hat underneath the name\n   248→\n   249→LEGEND LORE\n   250→\n   251→He serves the clery estate and their field is sterile, but a bloom of flowers grows in anyone that is planted in their field.\n   252→\n   253→They won the first harvest festival and he used the winnings for the next year, but he cheated. So he was cursed. Winklebottom now serves as the footstool of the Queen and will forever.\n   254→\n   255→Randall turns a sword into a circlet with a blacksmith, but she keeps the handle\n   256→\n   257→Fen's adventure at Storm Peaks\n   258→\n   259→The farmer's daughters are super hungover\n   260→\n   261→Back to the library at Storm's Peaks\n   262→\n   263→There's a ton of information here about Gardammon and the Zodiacs\n   264→\n   265→There's no information about the location of the zenith's, except for the one above Macchu, the Forest Zenith. But there was one that fell and became Tygras Logath.\n   266→\n   267→And notice, there's a HUGE crater where the last zenith fell at Tygras Logath\n   268→\n   269→Weirdly, there is no origin of the Zeniths\n   270→\n   271→And Xyn came by later to check out the book Fen did, but obviously, Fen already had it.\n   272→\n   273→And just as of note, there were a lot of fires down below in the last fire. And she's shut it down since the fire, even though a lot of students weren't visiting\n   274→\n   275→There's a god of destruction from the petal that has already been destroyed - the initiator of aboleth's and beholder's and mind flayer's. Therezadoon. The chained god of madness, he who walks below\n   276→\n   277→And there's a corpse of an earth elemental that makes up Bael'vun\n   278→\n   279→The harvest zenith was closed at the destruction of Uma, the matrim pact was made so that it would never been opened again. And the pact was made in the shadow of the zenith itself.\n   280→\n   281→Fen gets a 10% return on his investment at the Storm's Peaks bank. They were able to get military and financial investment\n   282→\n   283→Fen attempts to sell the Distillery's wine and beer at The Roost.\n   284→\n   285→Which he doesn't sell currently. Alcohol that is.\n   286→\n   287→He also is interested in Root Beer. Whatever that is.\n   288→\n   289→He suggests that we should open a tavern in the Roost.\n   290→\n   291→He would stay, but he's got to head back to the Harvest Festival asap.\n   292→\n   293→Brigit has an awkward interaction with Sam, Yvonne's husband apparently.\n   294→\n   295→Varis steps in and takes him to the windmill willow. And then creates a portal in the willow to send Sam through. And done.\n   296→\n   297→THE JOUST\n   298→\n   299→Randall loses the first bout\n   300→\n   301→WINS THE SECOND BOUT\n   302→\n   303→And loses the second bout\n   304→\n   305→Gaiger Kaine, The Griffin Rider. He'll be in the floating district of Monethern\n   306→\n   307→To be continued next time, around noonish.\n   308→"}]},"uuid":"0293fc96-c9f5-428f-a20d-f8b32b7715d0","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:07.405Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5e26b958-6c5e-4edc-8980-22670ff36390","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

This extraction synthesizes everything available from the local filesystem: directory structure, filenames, image names, handout text files, overarching DM notes, and recording filenames. A future pass should use the Google Docs API to pull the linked documents (gshortcut URLs point to Google Drive file IDs).\n    11→\n    12→---\n    13→\n    14→# ACT 3 — The Zenith Wars\n    15→\n    16→Act 3 spans Chapters 42-87 plus a Reunion session. The campaign splits into two concurrent party tracks: **Dwarves** (focused on Vag'Mahar and the Mountain's Zenith) and **Tieflings** (focused on DawnHaven, Cloud City, and the Fae/Harvest storyline). Around Chapter 67, the Tiefling track is renamed to **\"The Distillery\"** (referencing the Distillery location in Vartos), suggesting the Tiefling party relocates to that region.\n    17→\n    18→## Party Structure\n    19→\n    20→Act 3 runs two parallel parties playing alternating sessions:\n    21→- **Dwarves**: Daina, Bergs, Nocturnus, and allies — exploring Vag'Mahar, the Underdark, and the Mountain's Zenith\n    22→- **Tieflings** (later \"The Distillery\"): Nyx, Irethia, Tesca, Randal, and allies — exploring DawnHaven, Cloud City, the Fae, and eventually the Distillery in Vartos\n    23→\n    24→---\n    25→\n    26→## Chapter 42\n    27→- **Act**: 3\n    28→- **Party Tracks**: Dwarves, Tieflings\n    29→- **Key Evidence**: Tieflings folder contains image \"Grimnak Norman (Logath).jpg\"\n    30→- **NPCs Present**: Grimnak Norman — associated with Logath (Tygras Logath region)\n    31→- **Locations**: Likely Tygras Logath / DawnHaven region\n    32→- **Notes**: Grimnak Norman appears to be an orc NPC connected to the Logath conflict. The Orc-Human war is a central Act 3 plotline per the DM's conflict notes — the orc chieftain Logath made a deal with the same Fae demon as Tygras, creating an army of sorcerers threatening DawnPass.\n    33→\n    34→---\n    35→\n    36→## Chapter 43\n    37→- **Act**: 3\n    38→- **Party Tracks**: Dwarves, Tieflings\n    39→- **Key Evidence**: Empty folders only\n    40→- **Notes**: No locally-stored assets. {"parentUuid":"94ddb29c-2c09-42b0-9811-dfdd948cc934","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01F2wCGeJjTpZyCCNmGp8EwR","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"world-bible\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/world-bible.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov World Bible\n     8→\n     9→*Compiled from DM source files: Ideas/, Notes/, Places/, Visions/, and the pacing study.*\n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→\n    13→## Cosmology\n    14→\n    15→### The Gods and Primal Forces\n    16→\n    17→The cosmology of Mardov revolves around a central divine conflict between twin souls: **Vecna** (god of evil secrets, animancy) and **Sabine** (goddess of life, death, and fate). They are the original twin souls, sharing a domain over death. Sabine's additional dominion over life made Vecna jealous, and his campaign to steal her powers is the cosmic engine driving the entire narrative.\n    18→\n    19→**Key Deities Referenced:**\n    20→- **Sabine Ra** — Goddess of life, death, and fate. \"Your time comes when it comes.\" Her spirit was shattered; her gifts scattered across the world. She will return as a demi-lich through the merging of Ramses and Xerxes.\n    21→- **Vecna** — God of evil secrets and animancy. His avatar rules a parallel dimension (V-MARDOV). He is \"death incarnate knocking at the door.\"\n    22→- **The Raven Queen** — Goddess of death and necromancy. Her avatar's sacrifice creates a rift between worlds.\n    23→- **Bahamut** — God of justice, worshipped at Conquis Abbey and throughout Monethern. His highest priesthood is the Silver Scales: \"All that is Just is not Righteous! All that is Righteous is not Just!\"\n    24→- **Asmodeus** — God of tyranny and mind control magic.\n    25→- **Gruumsh** — God of destruction and chaos.\n    26→- **Tiamat** — Worshipped by the vampire queen of the Underdark, who gave her soul to Tiamat for an ascension ritual.\n    27→- **Pelor** — Temple in WindHaven (Field of Pelor).\n    28→- **Avandra** — Temple in WindHaven (Avandra's Gift) and Conquis Abbey (Gardens of Avandra).\n    29→- **Ioun** — Archive of Ioun at Conquis Abbey.\n    30→- **Erathis** — Workshop of Erathis in Tinkertown, Conquis Abbey.\n    31→- **Moradin** — God of dwarves, channeled at the Corpseforge in the Underdark.\n    32→\n    33→### The Planes\n    34→\n    35→**Material Plane:** Mardov — the continent, surrounded by seas. Contains the four Zeniths.\n    36→\n    37→**Outer Planes:**\n    38→- **7 Heavens**\n    39→- **9 Hells**\n    40→- **Astral Sea** — Contains \"Betwixt\" and \"The Veil\"\n    41→- **Eaden**\n    42→- **Fae Wildes** — Four courts: Brier Patch (Spring), Flowerfield (Summer), Reaper's Bounty (Autumn), Snowcrest Sanctum (Winter). Also contains the Glittering Run.\n    43→- **ShadowFell** — Contains RavenFall, Gate of the Broken, Path of the Watchers, Shadar's Gift, Vision Gate (with rooms for individual characters: Casaval, Daina, Stalwart)\n    44→- **Vodram** — Contains Valheim and Vodram's FeyWilde (Hengefield, Serpent Meadows, Ursa Rock)\n    45→- **Uvan**\n    46→- **The Moon** — Has its own location folder, implying it's reachable/significant.\n    47→- **V-MARDOV** — The parallel dimension where Vecna's avatar rules. Mirror of the material plane. Contains a \"Death's Zenith\" that mirrors the Forest's Zenith.\n    48→\n    49→### The Four Zeniths and Keystones\n    50→\n    51→The Zeniths are ancient nexus points of cosmic power. Each contains a **Heart** (a gemstone keystone) and an **Anchor** (a weapon tied to one of the Four Horsemen). Mordred's plan is to destroy all keystones, enabling V-MORDRED's invasion.\n    52→\n    53→| Zenith | Heart | Gemstone | Anchor | Horseman | Location | Vecna's Eye |\n    54→|--------|-------|----------|--------|----------|----------|-------------|\n    55→| **Forest's Zenith** | Heart of the Wilde | Emerald | Anchor of Death (obsidian sword) | Death | Machu, DawnHaven | Green Eye |\n    56→| **Mountain's Zenith** | Heart of the Forge | Garnet | Anchor of War (hammer-axe-javelin) | War | Vag Mahar, Monethern | Red Eye |\n    57→| **Winter's Zenith** | Heart of the Faith | Sapphire | Anchor of Pestilence (pan flute / turtle skeleton) | Pestilence | Bynar | Blue Eye |\n    58→| **Harvest's Zenith** | Heart of the Progenitors | Ruby | Anchor of Famine (Diadem of Dawn) | Famine | Tygras Logath, DawnHaven | Orange Eye |\n    59→\n    60→### The Valley of Anchors\n    61→Located in Var'taos ruins, this is \"the origin of magic.\" Anchors must be lifted and recharged here.\n    62→\n    63→### Guard'Daemon (Gard'Daemon)\n    64→Anagram for Armageddon. A prophesied apocalyptic event heralded by a bleeding/blood star visible around Mardov. \"Below the blood star begins the final war.\"\n    65→\n    66→### Twin Souls\n    67→A cosmic mechanic originating from Vecna and Sabine. When twin souls find each other, their power increases (boost the stat where their combined bonuses are highest). Known twin soul pairs:\n    68→- Vecna and Sabine (the originals)\n    69→- Daina and Bergs\n    70→- Nyx and her sister Xyn\n    71→\n    72→---\n    73→\n    74→## Geography\n    75→\n    76→### The Continent of Mardov\n    77→\n    78→Mardov is a continent with diverse regions connected by trade routes, mountain passes, rivers, and seas. The world has undergone cataclysmic reshaping — notably the destruction of Uma which created a desert, and the sealing of Tygras which created a crater.\n    79→\n    80→**Major Regions (from Places/ directory):**\n    81→\n    82→1. **DawnHaven** — The largest and most detailed region. A continent-spanning domain.\n    83→2. **Monethern** — A region centered on faith and dwarven culture.\n    84→3. **Vartos** — Home of the Magister's Circle, the Valley of Anchors, and the Distillery.\n    85→4. **Bynar** — A mountainous/arctic region, the Cradle mountains.\n    86→5. **Islands** — Including Qual'Xar and the Serpent Archipelago.\n    87→6. **Underdark** — The subterranean realm beneath the mountains.\n    88→7. **Outer Planes** — Extraplanar locations.\n    89→8. **The Moon** — An accessible location.\n    90→\n    91→*(See `locations-catalog.md` for the full directory breakdown.)*\n    92→\n    93→### Key Cities and Locations\n    94→\n    95→**WindHaven (DawnHaven):** A major city with districts including the Upper Crust, North Gate, temples to Bahamut/Pelor/Avandra, the Windswept Orphanage, and the Windmill Willow. Surrounded by the Game Trail with its Fringe Farms.\n    96→\n    97→**Dawnpass (DawnHaven):** Political capital. Contains Bridgetown with the Tower Bridge Refugees, Umbratown, Her Majesties Magistrates, Monastery of the Dawn, and Northpass with the Everstar Palace.\n    98→\n    99→**Machu (DawnHaven):** Home of the Forest's Zenith. Contains the Palace with its Old Guard royalty, the Circle of the Murder, and the City Outskirts above the Mortis River. The king is trapped in madness (like Jafar).\n   100→\n   101→**Cloud City / Storm Peaks (DawnHaven):** A massive floating location. Contains the 5 Cities of Giants (Cloudheim, Fireheim, Frostheim, Hillheim, Stormheim), plus Laputa Town, Nimbus, Sirrus, and the Palace of the 9 Winds.\n   102→\n   103→**Evallan Forest (DawnHaven):** The mystical forest. Contains the Emerald Guard, Grove of the Ancients with DoeFall Glade and Ursa Major.\n   104→\n   105→**Vag Mahar (Monethern):** The great dwarven city, divided into three sectors: Blackbeard (mines, military, wealthy estates), Brownbeard (docks, taverns, temples), and Redbeard (carpenters, residential, artisans). Contains the Mountain's Zenith within the Stone Sanctum. The sewer system has the Clocktower, Shed, and Lockbox. Pathway leads to the Standing Stones with Revolutionaries, the Frozen Forest, and ultimately to Monethern City.\n   106→\n   107→**Monethern City (Monethern):** A port city with the Floating Quarter, Fort Undercroft, Veritasium Palace with its Senatoriate, and the Spillway.\n   108→\n   109→**Conquis Abbey (Monethern):** A holy city dedicated to Bahamut. Districts: Scalestone Plaza (Platinum Temple of Bahamut, Fountain of Promise, River of Dragons), Lightwells, Ironwork Alley, Tinkertown, Court of the Creators, Villas of the Devoted, Archive of Ioun.\n   110→\n   111→**Magister's Circle (Vartos):** A magical academy/governing body. Contains the Circle with Eon's Sanctum, the Cabal of Horns, Candle Cove port, and shops.\n   112→\n   113→**The Distillery (Vartos):** Contains the Actual Size Tower, Claudere Keep, Faughart, and the Temple to the Zodiac.\n   114→\n   115→**Bynar / The Cradle:** Mountain region with Mt. Bynar, Mt. Dragonspine (FrostFang Village), and Shard Mountain. Home to the Winter's Zenith and the ruined dragon-kin tribe (Winter's Curators).\n   116→\n   117→**Underdark:** Multi-layered. D'Guarda Fortress (renovated into a stronghold), Da'Gahar, Green Tides of St Ashbury, Myconid Grove with the Lair of the Corpseforger. Lower Levels divided between Drow-controlled Kaivan Layer (Shrine of the Bat) and Mind Flayer-controlled Ulthradi Layer.\n   118→\n   119→---\n   120→\n   121→## Magic System\n   122→\n   123→### The Watchful Order\n   124→A guild of magicians who govern, teach, and occasionally put down forbidden forms of magic.\n   125→\n   126→**Common Magic (permitted):**\n   127→- Standard elemental magic (fireball, illusions)\n   128→- Standard cleric miracles (healing the sick)\n   129→\n   130→**Forbidden Magic:**\n   131→- **Necromancy** — Associated with the Raven Queen\n   132→- **Animancy** — Associated with Vecna\n   133→- **Tyranny/Charm/Mind Control** — Associated with Asmodeus\n   134→- **Destruction/Chaos** — Associated with Gruumsh\n   135→\n   136→### The Monkey's Paw Principle\n   137→In the laws of Mardov's universe, nothing can be gained for free. All magic items carry hidden costs:\n   138→- Bag of Holding: some small percentage of items get lost in the aether between uses\n   139→- Belt of Giant Strength: causes disadvantage on charm spells (giants are dumb)\n   140→\n   141→### Dark Magic\n   142→- Creatures wielding or created from dark magic are managed by the Watchful Order and destroyed if evil\n   143→- Most dark magicians stay hidden or are powerful enough to rival the Watchful Initiates\n   144→- Dark magic objects are worshipped by cults and mistaken for deities\n   145→\n   146→### Sources of Power (The Four Hearts)\n   147→Each Zenith channels a different fundamental force:\n   148→- **Arcane** (Heart of the Forge) — Raw magical energy, channeled through artifice\n   149→- **Radiant/Divine** (Heart of the Faith) — Raw divine power, channeled without a deity\n   150→- **Natural** (Heart of the Wilde) — Cosmic zodiac powers, source of druidcraft\n   151→- **Fae/Warlock** (Heart of the Progenitors) — Raw fae magics\n   152→\n   153→### The Covenants of Magic\n   154→After Tygras's superweapon destroyed Uma, covenants were established so that no being save for the Alabaster Seat may possess that amount of power again.\n   155→\n   156→---\n   157→\n   158→## The Overarching Story\n   159→\n   160→### Mordred's Plan\n   161→Mordred is the campaign's primary antagonist. His arc:\n   162→\n   163→1. **Origin:** Originally wanted to resurrect Sabine, his lost love. After her spirit was shattered, he shifted to collecting her gifts and controlling her power (this mirrors V-MORDRED in the parallel dimension).\n   164→2. **The Sacrifice:** Gets the Avatar of the Raven Queen to sacrifice her immortality, creating a rift between the material world and V-MARDOV (where Vecna's avatar rules).\n   165→3. **The Body:** Gave up his phylactery to take out the Artificer's Guild. This grants him Xerxes's body (corrupting the individual beings that make up Xerxes).\n   166→4. **The Endgame:** With his new body, Mordred has one goal: bring about the invasion by V-MORDRED by destroying all the keystones.\n   167→\n   168→### The Anchor System\n   169→Each Zenith houses both a Heart (power source) and an Anchor (weapon). The Anchors are pairs of swords (or other weapons) thrust into the center of each Zenith. When both the Forest and Desert anchors are severed, the Raven Queen's avatar will cease to exist, Sabine's crystalline heart will be removed from the adventurers, and V-MORDRED will be forced back to his realm.\n   170→\n   171→**Death's Anchor:** An obsidian sword in the Forest's Zenith. Originally black steel, now appears made of black glass. Carries a lingering curse with complex mechanics (charges, curse control on charisma saves).\n   172→\n   173→**Life's Anchor:** Paired with Death's Anchor in the desert (incomplete in notes).\n   174→\n   175→### The Three Prophecies\n   176→\n   177→**First Prophecy (Ruth, Argos):**\n   178→> \"The anchors fall, the realms asunder / Hearts of stone gathered from plunder / Death incarnate knocks at the door / Below the blood star begins the final war!\"\n   179→\n   180→**Second Prophecy (The Hags):**\n   181→> \"Double bubble toil and trouble / Gard'Daemon is neigh, the first zenith is rubble / Prepare the pestilence with children's souls / The husks remain until they're told / Double bubble toil and trouble / Anchors lifted and hearts are smuggled\"\n   182→\n   183→**Third Prophecy (Mia):**\n   184→Four verses mapping to four locations, each describing the quest to resolve one Zenith:\n   185→- **Harvest Zenith** (Tygras Logath): Rain on fallow harvest, Matrim pact fulfilled, echo of Tygras stilled\n   186→- **Winter Zenith** (Bynar): Snow over ruined tribe, serpent plagues the sea, curators' plea answered\n   187→- **Mountain Zenith** (Vag Mahar): Fire on forgotten forge, blood star found, new paragon crowned\n   188→- **Forest Zenith** (Machu): Sunlight warms forest gate, madness prisoner freed, star-crossed three find peace\n   189→\n   190→### The Matrim (Maturin) Pact\n   191→An ancient alliance between human sailors of old Uma, gnomes of Fae'ish, and Qual'Xar half-elves. They promised sanctuary to each other atop the Maturin Turtle. A tsunami in the wake of Tygras's explosion forced these pilgrims onto a giant turtle, creating a hidden capital — a buried pyramid with the Harvest's Zenith at its center.\n   192→\n   193→### The Blood Star\n   194→A recurring omen. One appeared when Tygras came to power. A meteorite that turns red and literally falls. It struck and killed King Thorson Bronzebeard II, last trueborn heir of Vag Mahar.\n   195→\n   196→---\n   197→\n   198→## Prophecies & Zeniths\n   199→\n   200→### The Tomb of the Last Curator"}]},"uuid":"c5e43eba-d952-435a-ab8c-94d3d813ad8b","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:08.808Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"94ddb29c-2c09-42b0-9811-dfdd948cc934","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"4b9b58f7-63a9-4b08-a65c-178135f10920","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WxCPn9ZLdvPHS6QEpPvwFR","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 66T - Brigit s Notes - The Harvest Festival\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_66T_-_Brigit_s_Notes_-_The_Harvest_Festival.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→TLDR\n     8→\n     9→We're moving to Roll20! Let's see how this goes\n    10→\n    11→We're in Windhaven. And because of that, we've got Stormpeak's north of us due to its falling 6 months ago.\n    12→\n    13→And our heroes were taking care of business in The Distillery.\n    14→\n    15→But, we're back now, in time for the Harvest Festival.\n    16→\n    17→Through a tree (for most of us) and a boat (Randall and Lorelei)\n    18→\n    19→We took care of some business once we got to Windhaven\n    20→\n    21→Of note: We're looking for a 6-fingered necromancer. There was one field where a girl was tending the dead, and her corpse flowers were being purchased by him\n    22→\n    23→Also, by upcasting a Greater Restoration, we can get 6 seconds of lucidity from someone under the drab plague.\n    24→\n    25→There is still a famine here, although it's not as bad as it used to be when the Storm was occurring. This year's harvest was okay.\n    26→\n    27→We meet our heroes in the Traveler's Pony.\n    28→\n    29→Inside the Traveler's Pony\n    30→\n    31→Sprocket walks out onto the patio and sees the Harvest Festival in full swing.\n    32→\n    33→Flying high above the city - a griffin and a rider.\n    34→\n    35→Fen hands out a note, explaining what happened in the Feywilde and his plan to thrwart Mr. R.\n    36→\n    37→We should watch out for Lorelei's friends, the 6 fingered man might be here.\n    38→\n    39→Out the front door!\n    40→\n    41→Alice - the proprietor. It's 5pm, the Queen is arriving at sunset, 7pm.\n    42→\n    43→But before we go, she needs help with something. Randall is going to participate in the contest to make the Queen smile.\n    44→\n    45→We head downstairs\n    46→\n    47→Downstairs we see - a gnomish woman, a man in a cloak, and the illustrator, Beren Rather.\n    48→\n    49→The gnomish woman comes up to Sprocket - the librarians have been called off work for the first time in history, they were the last two to enter the library, and just wanted to check to see if they knew anything strange. And she wanted to swing by and see where we were going.\n    50→\n    51→Leonidas delivered the promise stone, and then shut everyone out. The front door is missing.\n    52→\n    53→To the festival!\n    54→\n    55→Stalls with two gnome children flitting around\n    56→\n    57→We're checked for weapons to leave at the door.\n    58→\n    59→We see part of the crew of the Flooded Roots\n    60→\n    61→We see a group of bards dancing\n    62→\n    63→And a large male with a headdress with small wooden carvings\n    64→\n    65→Totems maybe? Owls. Pangolin.\n    66→\n    67→ZODIACS\n    68→\n    69→There's a roar of an owlbear that has been tamed, and children are joking around stealing its feathers\n    70→\n    71→And again, the same cloaked man handing out crystals\n    72→\n    73→And a fire dancer\n    74→\n    75→And a very long line gathering around the bonfire\n    76→\n    77→A prep stage with the griffin and his rider\n    78→\n    79→And the farmer's daughters with a mammoth and some other beasts\n    80→\n    81→And an elderly woman with cards\n    82→\n    83→What do we do?\n    84→\n    85→Sprocket looks around a place to set up the Distillery Stuff.\n    86→\n    87→Inside the stall with the gnome children\n    88→\n    89→We register our wares, setup next to The Green's\n    90→\n    91→Sprocket sets up dancing lights around the bottles and makes the stall look pretty\n    92→\n    93→A noble looking man comes up to try some wine. He's from Windhaven - Caesar, the Count.\n    94→\n    95→He doesn't like it though. There's some foam at the corner of his mouth?\n    96→\n    97→Note - marigold's are snakesbane. Thus why Fen doesn't like it either. So this dude is potentially associated with the Yanti!\n    98→\n    99→A woman appears wearing a blue dress, she has a cat weaving between her legs. She saw what happened and is dying to test it out.\n   100→\n   101→It's amazing! It tastes like summer!\n   102→\n   103→Harvest Day IPA - oranges and apricots. {"parentUuid":"8c972c59-9f9b-4d5d-bd42-cdae087551ac","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LBuNGNJ3tbZ5cryKhPnGuS","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 55T - Brigit s Notes - The Sky is Falling Arise Falek\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_55T_-_Brigit_s_Notes_-_The_Sky_is_Falling_Arise_Falek.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→Chapter 55T - Brigit’s Notes - The Sky is Falling/Arise Falek\n     8→\n     9→What are we doing, what do we know?\n    10→\n    11→The amulet is within the apparatus and it keeps Cloud City afloat. 3 systems keep it afloat: (1) The apparatus/The King, (2) Jack and (3) The Roost. The only one left is the amulet.\n    12→\n    13→The genasi, giants and orc invaders/fiends have all been fighting in Cloud City\n    14→\n    15→Reynaldine sent us here to get the amulet. Nyx did check and heard that he was going to fix everything. He absolutely believes that.\n    16→\n    17→Xyn wants to protect the amulet and get it for herself, so she can help revive Mr. Happy/Gabriel the Kind. Her whole goal is that she will also give it to Reynaldine but also try to lock him out of the zodiac. And she's interested in traveling to the Shadowfell.\n    18→\n    19→Options: leave the amulet, take it and keep it or take it and give it to Reynaldine/Xyn/someone.\n    20→\n    21→We're not sure what Reynaldine is going to do with it, but we're pretty sure it's not great. He's also been making pacts with people that we're not sure of what they are, but it's not great.\n    22→\n    23→Because he's fey he's going to keep his promise, but he's going to try to twist the words around as much as possible.\n    24→\n    25→And we know that he's working with the huge dragon and could also just charm us into doing whatever he wants.\n    26→\n    27→Note: the dragons have been watching over the apparatus.\n    28→\n    29→It seems like we're set on removing the amulet. Can we still save Cloud City?\n    30→\n    31→Can we bring the Roost back online, or get Keelick back somehow?\n    32→\n    33→We have magic create anything potion\n    34→\n    35→Can we create an amulet? We saw it making electrum, but that's nothing compared to the amulet.\n    36→\n    37→We could probably go get some of the King's essence from the Throne room and use it to run the apparatus.\n    38→\n    39→THE PLAN\n    40→\n    41→Leave the amulet with our allies except for Xyn\n    42→\n    43→Head to the Roost, try to get it back online\n    44→\n    45→Sidebar, try to save Euclid and Archimedes\n    46→\n    47→If that doesn't work, we should head to the throne room to try to capture the King's essence and use it to run the apparatus.\n    48→\n    49→And then once we know Cloud City is safe, we take the amulet with us.\n    50→\n    51→Somewhere.\n    52→\n    53→BEGINNING OF THE SESSION\n    54→\n    55→TLDR\n    56→\n    57→Varys found a lot of cool lore in the weird zodiac circle thing\n    58→\n    59→We saved Xyn with help from Leonidas and killed Tygras\n    60→\n    61→Starting\n    62→\n    63→Xyn hands Nyx the book - the origins of the genasi and just the biggest hug possible\n    64→\n    65→Mathia is close by being held by Stalwart and she is saying absolutely nothing. She has gold bands with an Ouroborus and the Knight of the Twisting Metal sigils on her tail.\n    66→\n    67→Fen tries to intimidate Mathia and Stalwart helps actually comes in with the muscle to get her to freak out.\n    68→\n    69→How many of you are there? Just her. And she's probably not lying.\n    70→\n    71→Fen scratches her with an arrow and it hurts her.\n    72→\n    73→The came here with the simple orcs (easy to control), they're the only force sent here to grab the amulet but more will follow if she fails. Sessatha needs me.\n    74→\n    75→Sessatha is the knight of the twisting meadows.\n    76→\n    77→Her kind have difficulty procreating, The apparatus (water) will raise Falack, the big ass serpent (zodiac). He will make more of us and they will ascend to their true purpose. By ruling everyone.\n    78→\n    79→Cool. Done with this. Stalwart stabs her through the facccccccccce.\n    80→\n    81→In the meantime while everyone loots bodies, Brigit talks to the Air Elemental\n    82→\n    83→He was summoned from the air plane. Which is why Brigit hasn't seen any of these before.\n    84→\n    85→The ring remains, Mathia has it. Fen takes the ring to release him of his bond. And Stalwart attacks the air elemental that wants to go home.\n    86→\n    87→We're going to get help from this Air Elemental and then eventually we'll let him die (when someone attunes to the ring)\n    88→\n    89→And now we've got to talk to Zephyron.\n    90→\n    91→He's still trying to keep Cloud City afloat. But if we can stop Blaine and Volcanis to stop then, we can get Zephyron to stop temporarily.\n    92→\n    93→We can write stuff on the paper, burn it and let Blaine know what's happening.\n    94→\n    95→Blaine says Xyn made a promise to Blaine, but she's not going to keep it. They're trying to wrestle control still and they're saying that something is happening at the palace and the genasi are moving somewhere quickly.\n    96→\n    97→Xyn is very confused that we're not sacrificing the city.\n    98→\n    99→\"Generally, killing people is bad\" -Nyx\n   100→\n   101→Brigit touches the amulet and is able to touch the amulet and turns into a water elemental. The locket glows blue, and Brigit condenses into water. She also gets a sense that the entirety of the hanging gardens in at my disposal - aka I know what's happening everywhere.\n   102→\n   103→And while Brigit is here she can proceed to steer the city.\n   104→\n   105→But it's super hard and this might take some time\n   106→\n   107→As she's thinking, she gets struck by fire and gets -90 HP.\n   108→\n   109→So yeet out of there. But if we can get them to stop fighting over the apparatus, then Brigit can move the city out of the way of Windhaven.\n   110→\n   111→Also Brigit didn't feel the presence of the water princess inside the amulet at all.\n   112→\n   113→It feels like a deep dark dungeon.\n   114→\n   115→Additionally Fen touches the amulet and allows it to be nondetected.\n   116→\n   117→There were definitely a few scrying it and there's a bit of a half laugh before the connection drop.\n   118→\n   119→Heading outside\n   120→\n   121→Volcanis and Blaine are waiting for us.\n   122→\n   123→We make many arguments - the city will fall, the apparatus will be destroyed, etc.\n   124→\n   125→We'll come back or send word in like 24 hours, and Randall gives him some Aboleth flesh so he can track us.\n   126→\n   127→To the boat\n   128→\n   129→The city is still leaning and falling, but only about an 8% grade.\n   130→\n   131→But all the acorn pods have shed from the roost and it looks like a barren shell.\n   132→\n   133→The bridges are still tenuous at best.\n   134→\n   135→We'll take the ship, but Liza allows Trixie and a few others stay and the rest of us go.\n   136→\n   137→Something happens and Stalwart challenges Liza to her ship. But they're going to be doing this later.\n   138→\n   139→What's up with Tezka? She's perfectly fine. Randall catches her up and lets her know that Tygras was there and that's why she was possessed. So wait, does that justify how Tieflings are treated? Wait. It was just me? So only some Tieflings are evil? Or possessable?\n   140→\n   141→Randall reaches forward with the broach and Tezka snaps and doesn't want it at all. She's done. She needs to study and is heading back to the monastery. If she's doesn't want to wear the broach, she has to go.\n   142→\n   143→Short rest\n   144→\n   145→To the Roost\n   146→\n   147→Varys sees fiends, but there are much less than we saw before.\n   148→\n   149→Mr. Tux is actually not at the Roost, Princess was left unconscious in front of the building.\n   150→\n   151→To Guest Services! (finally)\n   152→\n   153→Pre casts - Pass without a trace from Varys, Hail of Thornes from Fen and Conjure Animals from Brigit\n   154→\n   155→Randall stealthily push the doors that are push doors. The whole area is dark but Randall does not see anything in the room despite that Varys saying that there's two fiends.\n   156→\n   157→He places a lamp 30 ft in front of him with Mage Hand\n   158→\n   159→Varys steps in and holds his action\n   160→\n   161→Fen steps forward and looks for traps - the water surface illuminates\n   162→\n   163→Brigit steps forward, sends her wolves forward for recon, and then holds her action\n   164→\n   165→Stalwart sees nothing and casts Divine Sense\n   166→\n   167→And then points forward and says \"They're there\"\n   168→\n   169→Nyx moves forward, and can see the fiends with her Devil's Sight\n   170→\n   171→They look like the skeletons of large great apes but stretched for decades\n   172→\n   173→They are very interested in the sudden appearance of flame.\n   174→\n   175→And Nyx casts Hold Monster\n   176→\n   177→And one is held\n   178→\n   179→Randall moves his lantern forward and now everyone can see them\n   180→\n   181→Varys shoots one of them\n   182→\n   183→Brigit casts Ice Storm on them\n   184→\n   185→Varys hits the monsters as well, one was reaching up to the ceiling, but gets hit. It gets almost knocked backwards.\n   186→\n   187→Fen shoots forward and hits and hits\n   188→\n   189→One is held and the second one reaches upwards as we hear a click. Randall hears \"Sorry brother\" and then he retreats through the doorway itself.\n   190→\n   191→Brigit tries to dispel the magic, but doesn't, has her wolves run forward and try to open the door, turns into a wolf and borks forward\n   192→\n   193→Stalwart tries to bash open the door, hits the held person and crits and smites and kill it.\n   194→\n   195→The fiend is crumpled and crushed\n   196→\n   197→Stalwart tries again and is able to smash the door open.\n   198→\n   199→Nyx eldritch blasts! But misses and hits!\n   200→\n   201→The water rises and is overflowing out the doorway downward\n   202→\n   203→We get pushed down toward the door we're heading for. One wolf pushes Stalwart down the hallway, but he manages to stop himself.\n   204→\n   205→Randall's eldritch blasts the switch AND TURNS IT OFF. There's still a steady stream headed downwards\n   206→\n   207→Varys can see the baboon but is on the other side of the door, and uses his arrows to push the baboon with force damage.\n   208→\n   209→The baboon emaciates and distigrates in front of Stalwart.\n   210→\n   211→End of combat\n   212→\n   213→We run through and close the door.\n   214→\n   215→We get the sense that fiends are coming up.\n   216→\n   217→We're waiting.\n   218→\n   219→We hear voices underneath the water, but can hear a cackle of a voice. Cloven feet along gravel and stone and others are following - Fen knows that this is the guy that messed with his head. Randall hears in Abysal - the book wasn't there, but we managed to find the owl and his pet owl. We'll have to figure out what happened to Tygras, but for now they must return home.\n   220→\n   221→We prepare. Varys goes ethereal to surprise them with the water behind the door.\n   222→\n   223→Nyx looks at the book\n   224→\n   225→There's lots of handwriting - it used to be in Euclid's study but he said that Reynaldine stole it. Nyx looks at the Progenitors - Xyn's handwriting.\n   226→\n   227→Nyx is able to find a specific ritual - the progenitor's were the original inhabitants of Mardov. They were effectively the curators (Cloyma maybe?) And before the elemental war, they disappeared. This ritual is the ritual Reynaldine wants. And it is a prophecy.\n   228→\n   229→The Prophecy of the Harvest Zenith\n   230→"}]},"uuid":"952dbf0a-bdcb-483e-b076-8cf7c0dfa006","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:21.990Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8c972c59-9f9b-4d5d-bd42-cdae087551ac","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"7cc1ce52-595f-4621-8c9e-f6426a4f5c1f","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01U5tFhYdQrFCSurJ2P8nefR","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"canon-reconciliation\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/canon-reconciliation.md\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→# Mardov Pilot A -- Canon Reconciliation\n     8→\n     9→**Generated**: 2026-03-17\n    10→**Sources Cross-Referenced**:\n    11→- DM chapter summaries (`chapters-act1-2.md`, Chapters 0-2)\n    12→- DM world bible (`world-bible.md`)\n    13→- DM character profiles (`characters-pcs.md`)\n    14→- Pilot story bible (`pilot-story-bible.md`, Panels 1-80)\n    15→- Player summaries 0-1, 2, 3-4 (`player-notes-summaries.md`)\n    16→- Brigit's session notes, Chapter 5+ (`player-notes-brigit.md`)\n    17→- Brigit's backstory (`Backstories Compiled.docx`)\n    18→\n    19→---\n    20→\n    21→## Part 1: Contradictions Between DM and Player Sources\n    22→\n    23→### 1.1 The Kobold Provocation -- Who Started It?\n    24→\n    25→**DM Version (Chapter 0 summary):**\n    26→> The Kobolds are chanting \"SCARLET, SCARLET, SCARLET\" but are initially just staring. They are \"provoked\" but no specific instigator is named.\n    27→\n    28→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n    29→> \"Daina (not Sprocket), splashed sewer sludge on one of them. Provoked and pissed the Kobolds readied for attack.\"\n    30→\n    31→The parenthetical \"(not Sprocket)\" suggests the table argued about who did it. The player summary names Daina definitively.\n    32→\n    33→**Pilot Version:** The pilot omits the provocation entirely. Stalwart's Draconic speech is the first interaction (Panels 51-54).\n    34→\n    35→**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot version stands. The kobolds are a threat to be defused, not a comedy beat about sludge-splashing. However, the detail that the kobolds were *passive until provoked* is useful atmosphere -- they were watching, chanting, following. Use the eerie chanting of \"SCARLET, SCARLET, SCARLET\" in the audio design for Panels 51-52. It is too good to waste.\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→### 1.2 Jorgen's Role -- Rescuer or Trap-Layer?\n    40→\n    41→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n    42→> Jorgen is \"a human ranger in a gold tabard\" who \"comes sprinting in pursued by flying kobolds.\" He is an established Brigade member (gold tunic = veteran). After the kobold fight, Finkle assigns the sewer mission.\n    43→\n    44→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n    45→> Jorgen \"claimed that the Kobolds had followed him across town from the sewers and had killed his previous set of recruits.\" Additionally, \"Dallor Dallorbill was able to swipe a note written to Jorgen from Finkle\" listing the recruits' names, classes, and arrival time -- implying Finkle and Jorgen are coordinating.\n    46→\n    47→The player version adds the stolen note detail, which is critical: Finkle knew exactly who was coming and when. The note makes Jorgen complicit from the beginning -- he is not a random ranger, he is bait.\n    48→\n    49→**Pilot Version:** Jorgen arrives at the tavern (Panel 43) bleeding, with shapes behind him. He leads the party into the sewers and betrays them in the ritual chamber (Panels 67-68).\n    50→\n    51→**CANON FOR PILOT**: Merge. Jorgen's tavern arrival should include the stolen note as a subtle plant. Possible implementation: after the tavern fight (Panel 45 aftermath), Dallor (established as a pickpocket in Panel 31c) produces a note he lifted from the ranger. The note lists their names. Nobody on screen knows what this means yet. The audience will understand on rewatch after the betrayal.\n    52→\n    53→---\n    54→\n    55→### 1.3 Finkle's Introduction vs. the Pilot's Recruitment\n    56→\n    57→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n    58→> Finkle is the Brigade's COO. He asks \"mysterious questions about ancient cults in the area, particularly the Raven Queen cult.\" He reveals that cult members \"use necromancy and animancy, view death as a steroid, and mark themselves with symbols under their eyelids.\"\n    59→\n    60→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n    61→> \"Broden Rootbeard received an inspiration event point and the following information about the Raven Queen Cult\" -- confirming one player was particularly engaged with this lore drop.\n    62→\n    63→**Brigit's Chapter 5 Notes:**\n    64→> \"He's why we don't remember signing up for the brigade\" -- Brigit's notes reveal that Mordred manipulated the party into joining. They did not volunteer.\n    65→\n    66→**Pilot Version:** Finkle is a \"gnome bureaucrat/drill sergeant\" (Panels 35-38) running a recruitment yard. The Raven Queen cult questions are not present. The pilot treats recruitment as straightforward military onboarding.\n    67→\n    68→**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot's streamlined Finkle is correct for pacing. However, the \"not remembering signing up\" detail from Brigit is enormously important for later episodes. Plant it subtly: in Panel 34 (recruits waiting), one character could have a confused expression or whisper \"Did you sign up for this?\" to another. This becomes a thread for Episode 2.\n    69→\n    70→---\n    71→\n    72→### 1.4 The Sewer Entrance -- Sealed In or Led Down?\n    73→\n    74→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n    75→> The party goes to the sewers to collect four sources of ancient magic. The mission is framed as legitimate Brigade work.\n    76→\n    77→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n    78→> \"Jorgen pulled a lever on the other end of the hallway and entered the room. Immediately upon entry the door slammed shut and the sewer entrance was sealed. The lever when pulled later lead to nothing, as if it had already served its purpose.\" Jorgen also separated from the party, \"slipping his way out of the sight of the Kobolds.\"\n    79→\n    80→This detail is damning: Jorgen seals them in and slips away. The lever is a one-way mechanism. The party is being herded.\n    81→\n    82→**Pilot Version:** The descent (Panels 48-61) shows Jorgen leading the party down. No explicit sealing mechanism is shown.\n    83→\n    84→**CANON FOR PILOT**: Include the sealed-in moment. After Panel 49 (sewer entrance), add a sound cue: a stone mechanism grinding shut behind them. Jorgen does not react. Nobody notices. The audience does.\n    85→\n    86→---\n    87→\n    88→### 1.5 The Ritual Chamber -- What Was Actually in It?\n    89→\n    90→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n    91→> \"An ancient ritual site.\" The party needs to \"collect four sources of ancient magic to disperse the kobold infestation.\" The DM frames the mission as pest control.\n    92→\n    93→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n    94→> The ritual chamber has \"a massive open chamber with a steeped roof and four endless waterfalls of orange, red, blue and green liquid/pseudo-liquid into a grate below. In the center of the room on top of a massive gear-like pedestal sits a Phylactery of the Raven Queen. Under the pedestal is grooved with tracks from the center to each of the 4 streams. Additionally, there are mysterious markings on the tracks. Along the walls to the room are a total of 12 tunnels to Kobold nests.\"\n    95→\n    96→**Player Version (Summary 2):**\n    97→> Finkle is revealed as \"actually a Lich named Mordred.\" The phylactery \"contains a Demi-Lich of current unknown origin\" (later revealed as Sabine). The four liquids are more precisely described: \"RED blood, hot to the touch; ORANGE mercury mixed with molten iron; BLUE liquid souls; GREEN poison, corrosive like acid.\"\n    98→\n    99→**Pilot Version:** The ritual chamber (Panel 64) has four waterfalls but recasts them as: blood-red (sacrifice), acid-green (pestilence), mercury-orange (war), soul-blue (death). No phylactery is visible in the pilot. The altar bears the Raven Queen's crest. The sealed steel door with concentric rings is the focus (Panels 71-74).\n   100→\n   101→**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot's four-waterfall color system is the adaptation layer. The actual game had the waterfalls as *ingredients* for a necromantic ritual. The pilot reframes them as *symbolic* of the four horsemen/anchors. This is a deliberate creative choice that maps to the endgame Zenith system. Keep the pilot version. The phylactery and Mordred reveal are Episode 2 material.\n   102→\n   103→---\n   104→\n   105→### 1.6 The Control Room and Half-Ogres\n   106→\n   107→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n   108→> Half the party \"find Jorgen and Finkle's Half Ogre henchmen Stromble and Grom or Gromble and Strom in a control room pulling levers (priming the ritual chamber).\"\n   109→\n   110→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n   111→> Same detail, but adds: \"They are caught off guard by the sudden arrival and struggle to explain why they are here (clearly lying).\"\n   112→\n   113→**Brigit's Chapter 5 Notes:**\n   114→> References \"Stromble and Grom\" from earlier sessions, confirming their presence.\n   115→\n   116→**Pilot Version:** The half-ogres are present as Finkle's bodyguards in the recruitment yard (Panels 35, 38) but do NOT appear in the underground sequence. The control room is absent.\n   117→\n   118→**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot is correct to exclude the control room. The underground sequence in the pilot is about atmosphere and discovery, not mechanical puzzle-solving. The half-ogres serve better as foreshadowing in the yard -- their presence hints at \"why does a gnome bureaucrat need two half-ogre bodyguards?\"\n   119→\n   120→---\n   121→\n   122→### 1.7 The Ceiling Trap and \"KNEEL\"\n   123→\n   124→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n   125→> A descending ceiling trap with a 180-second timer. \"RUN LEFT\" scratched into the ceiling leads to a passage through the wall.\n   126→\n   127→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n   128→> A blood rune button, 180-second countdown, the ceiling descending. \"'RUN LEFT' prompted Stalwart, Nyx, Daina and Brigit, to run into the left wall and fade through just in time.\" Meanwhile others \"had to be fought back\" after the ceiling descended.\n   129→\n   130→**Pilot Version (Panel 59):**\n   131→> \"KNEEL\" written in blood on the ceiling, seen from the character's POV looking up as the ceiling descends. This is the pilot's most physically oppressive moment.\n   132→\n   133→**CANON FOR PILOT**: \"KNEEL\" is better than \"RUN LEFT\" for the adaptation. \"RUN LEFT\" is a game mechanic hint. \"KNEEL\" is thematic -- it ties to Mordred's desire for submission, it works visually (single word, blood, descending ceiling), and it echoes across the series whenever someone demands submission. Use KNEEL.\n   134→\n   135→---\n   136→\n   137→### 1.8 The Betrayal -- Finkle/Mordred vs. Jorgen\n   138→\n   139→**DM Version (Chapter 2):**\n   140→> The betrayer is *Finkle*, who is revealed as Mordred the Lich. Jorgen is his mercenary/kobold trainer, ultimately disposable. Mordred commands Stalwart to kill Jorgen because \"he had outlived his usefulness.\"\n   141→\n   142→**Player Version (Summary 2):**\n   143→> Same. Finkle = Mordred. Jorgen is a tool. The real threat was the gnome all along.\n   144→\n   145→**Pilot Version:**\n   146→> Jorgen is the betrayer (Panels 67-68). No Mordred reveal. No Finkle-is-a-lich twist. The pilot ends on the sealed door, not the ritual completion.\n   147→\n   148→**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot is correct. The Mordred/Finkle reveal is the Episode 2 cliffhanger. The pilot's Jorgen betrayal is a *different* kind of twist -- not \"the friendly gnome is actually the big bad\" but \"the competent rescuer led you here on purpose.\" This works better for a first episode because it teaches the audience rule #1 of Mardov: trust is earned, not granted by role.\n   149→\n   150→---\n   151→\n   152→### 1.9 Stalwart's Draconic Speech -- Intimidation or Kinship?\n   153→\n   154→**DM Version (Chapter 0):**\n   155→> Stalwart \"intimidated them in Draconic.\"\n   156→\n   157→**Player Version (Summary 0-1):**\n   158→> \"Stalwart Little stepped in to intimidate them in Draconic. Something about the Dragonborn was able to keep the Kobolds at bay despite their eerie chanting.\"\n   159→\n   160→**Pilot Version (Panels 53-54):**\n   161→> \"Stalwart's voice in Draconic: deep, resonant, DIFFERENT from human speech.\" The framing is heritage, not intimidation. \"It is not a trick -- it is heritage asserting itself.\"\n   162→\n   163→**CANON FOR PILOT**: The pilot's framing is richer. Stalwart speaks Draconic not as a combat tactic but as a statement of identity. The kobolds respond to kinship, not fear. This establishes Stalwart as a leader whose authority comes from who he is, not what he threatens.\n   164→\n   165→---\n   166→\n   167→### 1.10 Missing Events -- Present in One Source But Not the Other\n   168→\n   169→**In DM notes but NOT in pilot:**\n   170→- Broden/Brotor Rootbeard (completely absent from pilot)\n   171→- Corrumpere the tiefling sorcerer (absent)\n   172→- Nocturnus Wayne (present in game Ch 0-2, absent from pilot party)\n   173→- Bergs (absent from pilot party -- introduced later in campaign)\n   174→- Daina being captured by Mordred (Ch 3-4 material)\n   175→- The gladiator arena (Ch 3-4)\n   176→- Xerxes and the Artificer's Guild rescue (Ch 3-4)\n   177→- Sabine's crystalline hearts / \"the awakened\" concept (Ch 2-4)\n   178→\n   179→**In player notes but NOT in DM notes:**\n   180→- Dallor stealing the note from Jorgen (player version adds this)\n   181→- The \"not remembering signing up\" detail (Brigit's notes)\n   182→- The specific \"SCARLET, SCARLET, SCARLET\" chanting (player summary)\n   183→- The comedy of the names \"Gromble/Stromble and Strom/Grom\" (player running gag)\n   184→\n   185→**In pilot but NOT in either source:**\n   186→- Conquis Abbey (relocated from Argos)\n   187→- The god-killer forge sequence (Panels 15-19, entirely new)\n   188→- The riders/summons framing (Acts 1-2, entirely new)\n   189→- The notice board / refugee queue (Panels 12-14, new world-building)\n   190→- The sealed steel door as a cliffhanger (the game's sealed door exists but the pilot elevates it to the central mystery)\n   191→\n   192→---\n   193→\n   194→## Part 2: Player Details the DM Missed\n   195→\n   196→### 2.1 Brigit's Internal Voice\n   197→\n   198→Brigit's notes are written in real-time during play, which gives them an immediacy the DM summaries lack.\n   199→\n   200→**Moments of Brigit thinking out loud:**"}]},"uuid":"98fbebbb-a4f8-4988-840a-8189f6f0dcac","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:22.191Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7cc1ce52-595f-4621-8c9e-f6426a4f5c1f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"a34faf71-8600-4faf-ad5d-d1f7f37723b6","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_019XSUz193wS5PfXtZC5mr57","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore\n     3→title: \"Cosmology\"\n     4→---\n     5→\n     6→# Cosmology of Mardov\n     7→\n     8→## The Divine Conflict — The Cosmic Engine\n     9→\n    10→The entire world of [[Mardov]] is driven by one act of divine jealousy. [[Vecna]] and [[Sabine Ra]] were the original **twin souls** — both gods of death, but [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] also ruled life and fate. [[Vecna]]'s envy of that additional dominion is the cosmic engine driving every conspiracy in Mardov.\n    11→\n    12→[[Sabine Ra|Sabine]]'s avatar (the [[Raven Queen]]) founded two colleges of magic:\n    13→- The **Circle of the Murder** (necromancy/death) — entrusted to her teacher [[Mordred|Ramses]]\n    14→- The **[[Artificer's Guild]]** (animancy/life) — entrusted to her husband [[Xerxes]]\n    15→\n    16→[[Mordred|Ramses]] grew jealous and attempted to grant both himself and [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] eternal life through lichdom. When [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] rejected the ritual, [[Mordred|Ramses]] forced it on her. [[Sabine Ra|Sabine]] starved her lich form by refusing to consume souls, crystallizing into a Demi-Lich trapped inside a phylactery for centuries.\n    17→\n    18→This created the fracture that [[Vecna]] exploits across the entire campaign.\n    19→\n    20→## The Gods\n    21→\n    22→### Greater Deities\n    23→\n    24→| Deity | Domain | Status | Notes |\n    25→|-------|--------|--------|-------|\n    26→| [[Sabine Ra]] | Life, Death, Fate | Shattered — fragments in each party member | The original twin soul with Vecna. \"Your time comes when it comes.\" |\n    27→| [[Vecna]] | Evil Secrets, Animancy | Active — rules [[V-Mardov]] | Commands all four Zenith operations. \"Death incarnate knocking at the door.\" |\n    28→| [[Raven Queen]] | Death, Necromancy | Avatar sacrificed | Sabine's avatar. Founded Circle of Murder + Artificer's Guild. |\n    29→| [[Bahamut]] | Justice | Active | Worshipped at [[Conquis Abbey]]. Silver Scales priesthood. |\n    30→| [[Asmodeus]] | Tyranny | Killed by [[Josh Coolbreeze]] (Ch. 136) | Three specters escaped at death — one entered [[Nocturnus Wayne]]. |\n    31→| [[Tiamat]] | Chromatic Dragons | Avatar Awakened | Avatar is [[Ashara Everstar]] ([[Irethia]]'s template). |\n    32→| Therzadun | Destruction | Sealed behind Iron Doors | Agents: [[Reginald Providence]] (killed), Silence/Timothy (vessel). |\n    33→| [[lolth]] | Deception | Active | Made deal with [[Raven Queen]] against [[Asmodeus]]. \"Two Truths and a Lie.\" |\n    34→| [[Graz'zt]] | Pleasure | Defeated at Stone Table | Held [[Heart of the Progenitors]]. Attempted Apotheosis by proxy. |\n    35→| Gruumsh | Destruction, Chaos | Referenced | Forbidden magic associated with his teachings. |\n    36→| Pelor | Sun | Active | Temple in [[windhaven]] (Field of Pelor). |\n    37→| Avandra | Change | Active | Temple in [[windhaven]] and [[Conquis Abbey]]. |\n    38→| Ioun | Knowledge | Active | Archive at [[Conquis Abbey]]. |\n    39→| Erathis | Civilization | Active | Workshop in Tinkertown, [[Conquis Abbey]]. |\n    40→| Moradin | Dwarves | Active | Channeled at the Corpseforge in the Underdark. |\n    41→| Jergal | Death (ordered) | Active | Three races of elves: Machu ([[Vecna]]), Drow ([[Raven Queen]]), Eavallan (Jergal). |\n    42→\n    43→### Celestials\n    44→\n    45→| Name | Role |\n    46→|------|------|\n    47→| Gabriel the Kind | Archangel of kindness. [[Reynaldine]]'s beloved. Swapped with [[Graz'zt]] at the Stone Table. |\n    48→| Dominel the Just | Angel Seal holder. Hates Barachiel. |\n    49→| Barachiel the Righteous | Angel Seal holder. Hates Dominel. |\n    50→| [[Xyn]] | [[Nyx Khamestria|Nyx]]'s twin sister. The Owl's Chosen. |\n    51→\n    52→## The Progenitors\n    53→\n    54→Ancient cosmic beings connected to the natural order. The **Children of the Forest** are their remnants:\n    55→- **Original**: Bugz, Cycle (lost to [[Xerxes]]), Frost, Haystack\n    56→- **Reborn**: Bark, Blossom, Breeze, Chlora, Lumin, Scale (Yuan-Ti possessed), Sylva, Sprig\n    57→\n    58→## The Four Zeniths\n    59→\n    60→Ancient nexus points of cosmic power. Each contains a Heart (gemstone keystone) and an Anchor (weapon tied to one of the Four Horsemen).\n    61→\n    62→| Zenith | Heart | Gemstone | Anchor | Horseman | Location | Eye of Vecna |\n    63→|--------|-------|----------|--------|----------|----------|-------------|\n    64→| [[Forest's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Wilde]] | Emerald | [[Anchor of Death]] (obsidian sword) | Death | [[machu]], [[DawnHaven]] | Green |\n    65→| [[Mountain's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Forge]] | Garnet | [[Anchor of War]] (hammer-axe-javelin) | War | [[Vag'Mahar]], [[monethern]] | Red |\n    66→| [[Winter's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Faith]] | Sapphire | [[Anchor of Pestilence]] (pan flute / Horn of Winter) | Pestilence | [[bynar]] | Blue |\n    67→| [[Harvest's Zenith]] | [[Heart of the Progenitors]] | Ruby | [[Anchor of Famine]] ([[Diadem of Dawn]]) | Famine | [[Tygras Logath]], [[DawnHaven]] | Orange |\n    68→\n    69→## The Raven Queen\n    70→\n    71→The [[Raven Queen]] holds 3 pieces of her heart, returned by past heroes:\n    72→\n    73→1. Tiny Rick\n    74→2. Brotor Rootbeard\n    75→3. Genevieve (delivered by Glade)\n    76→\n    77→Heroes who currently carry fragments of her heart:\n    78→\n    79→| Hero | Zodiac |\n    80→|------|--------|\n    81→| [[Dallor Dallorbill]] | [[Serpent Zodiac|Serpent]] |\n    82→| Brotor Rootbeard | — |\n    83→| Genevieve | [[Doe Zodiac|Doe]] |\n    84→| Tiny Rick | — |\n    85→| [[Stalwart Little Goldentooth]] | [[Mongoose Zodiac|Mongoose]] |\n    86→| [[Nyx Khamestria]] | [[Owl Zodiac|Owl]] |\n    87→| [[Sprocket Overspark]] | [[Rabbit Zodiac|Rabbit]] |\n    88→| [[Daina Fireforge]] | [[Wolverine Zodiac|Wolverine]] |\n    89→| [[Nocturnus Wayne]] | [[Bat Zodiac|Bat]] |\n    90→| [[Brigit Wildfire]] | [[Stag Zodiac|Stag]] |\n    91→\n    92→## The Elements, the Fey, and the Dragons\n    93→\n    94→Each element has its own Fey realm alignment, and every Dragonborn/Dragon falls upon this spectrum:\n    95→\n    96→### Winter\n    97→\n    98→- **Fey Leader:** [[Grumblesin of the Winter Court]]\n    99→- **Dragonborn:** White, Blue, Silver\n   100→  - **Chieftain:** Nic"}]},"uuid":"4eda0a8a-f189-401b-91db-f8d248edaa8f","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:22.806Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a34faf71-8600-4faf-ad5d-d1f7f37723b6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}

{"parentUuid":"cbe44c32-61b8-4ecb-91fd-1bb9be6d2133","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"616419db-f8ea-4cbf-8b1b-8e3d3d602562","agentId":"aec734cdb1eeb4deb","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SZfvWydMtQFdWYySAn4uFM","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→---\n     2→type: lore-extraction\n     3→title: \"Chapter 45T Brigit s Notes A Barrel Full of Kids Teach a Man to Fish\"\n     4→source: [\"NFS/lore-extraction/Chapter_45T___Brigit_s_Notes___A_Barrel_Full_of_Kids_Teach_a_Man_to_Fish.txt\"]\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→Notes from Chapter 45T – Brigit’s Notes – A Barrel Full of Kids/Teach a Man to Fish\n     8→\n     9→Tezka and Randall are in a bar\n    10→\n    11→Tezka is disguised as a merchant, and a fashionable turban with blue eyes.\n    12→\n    13→Randall takes his drink, tastes like crap, so he knocks it over. It falls into a series of cracks and he receives a message: \"Get your things, head to the kitchen, and wait 5 minutes to make sure you're not followed. Ask for Mack at the freezer door.\"\n    14→\n    15→Head to the Kitchen. The bartender offers a free round of drink to the whole bar, causing mayhem, and Tezka and Randall slip into the kitchen.\n    16→\n    17→They hear a creak of a door handle and a lock, so possibly someone tried to get in and was unable to.\n    18→\n    19→After 5 minutes, the fire flares up and turns from red to blue.\n    20→\n    21→The blue flames come out, and from the fireplace itself, the illusion of a roaring fire is gone, and there is a spiral staircase leading downward. A red tiefling man appears, Mack.\n    22→\n    23→Tezka and Randall aren't from here and want to know what's going on in these parts. But, it's weird that they’re not Tieflings? And Tezka un-costumes and says hello, vouches for Randall. They have arrived in Umbertown (the underground of the cities)\n    24→\n    25→He's pretty suspicious. What's the worst tree in the world? The Birthwright (The one in the Magister's Circle)\n    26→\n    27→Dawnpass is pretty bad - the Tiefling's had a schism there and now there's two groups.\n    28→\n    29→Note: Here in windhaven, look out for a fishing vessel on the sign, that means it's our place.\n    30→\n    31→Downstairs: pretty much the same footprint, they head to a common room where there is one big table for everyone.\n    32→\n    33→They meet a red-headed lady. Randall and Tezka are looking to help out because of the exploits they've recently been in.\n    34→\n    35→Scoles of Folic\n    36→\n    37→The tieflings are missing kids. First they went for the orphanages, of goblins, of humans, of dragonborn, of everyone. They found a masquerade mask (The Autumn Court?).\n    38→\n    39→She calls out to Esmerelda, a red skinned Tiefling girl. She was outside, and saw three people that looked like humans, but they had yellow eyes with slits. They tried to kidnap her. Eventually she got picked up by Mack, and she says they had the mask that they were holding.\n    40→\n    41→Nyx, Sprocket, Stalwart and Varys have finished the trial and headed to the Winchester to have a pint and wait for things to blow over.\n    42→\n    43→It's quite a bit rainy outside despite the sun being out - The Clouded City is rolling in and bringing a torrent with us, but it's not quite there yet.\n    44→\n    45→There's only one bar, and it caters to a specific kind of clientele. This is a brothel.\n    46→\n    47→TLDR: yesterday they allegedly committed a crime at the windmill, but today it is a different day. Keelick is leaving at 3:30 and is heading to where he needs to be to leave at 5.\n    48→\n    49→There are 2 separate bars in this bar: rounded ear (human), and daggered ear (elven) section.\n    50→\n    51→But it all depends on their preference (nudge nudge)\n    52→\n    53→Also, Varys is looking for Evandra's blessing, muddled wine.\n    54→\n    55→<Insert discussion on inter-sex rules here> The main reason for this separation is because Windhaven has both humans and elves that reside here (in basically equal numbers)\n    56→\n    57→Everyone heads to the elven side:\n    58→\n    59→A scuffle is happening in the human half of the bar - sound of boots rushing away from the human doorway. But our travelers are heading to the elven side. Varys orders an Evandra's blessing, a half-elven bartender serves him - wearing various colors of shirts that inform the patrons if they are available.\n    60→\n    61→RED - no, YELLOW - you can try but you might end up being paid, and GREEN - for purchase.\n    62→\n    63→The elven half is pretty barren, but there are a few patrons milling about.\n    64→\n    65→One lady is drinking at the bar and has a sullen look in her eyes.\n    66→\n    67→Sprocket orders a pint of ale, A PINT.\n    68→\n    69→Stalwart picks up a mask from a lady who drops something, but she keeps running. And a horde of angry human women come running out - \"AND STAY OUT. Shouldn't have messed with farmer's daughter!\" And they head to the other bar.\n    70→\n    71→Stalwart heads to the north gate.\n    72→\n    73→Nyx decides that she wants answers, and heads to the tree. She's got some questions for Leonidas, who's at the top of the tree, and she's going to head there. (Leonidas is kind of the mayor here, he runs the town).\n    74→\n    75→And they finish their drinks and leave.\n    76→\n    77→Back to the Tieflings!\n    78→\n    79→As Esmeralda is telling her story, Tezka and Randall hear a chorus of boots and that someone has tried to get in. They also notice that there's some magic circles on the floor and one is activated - by the grey stuff dropped before.\n    80→\n    81→She believes the Scales of Folic have been here for a bit - just about the time the famine hit. The Nume family are nobles and she believes they are a rival organization - she has a hunch they're not human.\n    82→\n    83→There was a masquerade going on yesterday - where the king of winterhaven was at. Mrs. Mathia Nume went into her study and she eavesdropped and heard 'Scales of Folic'.\n    84→\n    85→The weather's going to be pretty bad, and you're going to need the farmer's daughters to go anywhere in this storm. You've got to pay them, they're mercenaries.\n    86→\n    87→Headed back upstairs\n    88→\n    89→Tezka quickly disguises herself again.\n    90→\n    91→Outside the door, they try to push it open, but something is blocking the door. On the floor is Mack, dead. His eyes have a faint flowing green/yellow pus. Randall starts to look him over, and the barkeep hears, Dalila and comes over to see what's up.\n    92→\n    93→The farmer's daughters are still here, and they're quite animated.\n    94→\n    95→They saw what happened and ran out the people that were trying to get into the kitchen.\n    96→\n    97→Randall and Tezka need to get out in the storm and heard they're the best.\n    98→\n    99→They need to check out the docks - there are two ships - the one that never leaves and the other one.\n   100→"}]},"uuid":"e1cf6681-bb0d-4812-bd1d-1bf206f5e032","timestamp":"2026-03-19T05:59:30.979Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cbe44c32-61b8-4ecb-91fd-1bb9be6d2133","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"e89c0432-c0b0-471a-97e7-f75f9aa647b4","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"stateful-seeking-melody"}
